Result: 仿真验证三项预测：(P1) 在 d=20 下，对齐排序为 Ideal > ToM-2 > Mean > EVR，所有教师引导条件均优于学习者引导基线；(P2) d 从 20 增至 40 时，理想教师与 EVR 在相同轮次下的差距明显扩大，验证 √d 尺度律；(P3) 对齐曲线与教师模型误差（EMD）曲线镜像，无陈述时误差峰值明显，mean 陈述降低误差，ToM-2 陈述使误差单调下降。ToM-2 陈述预算 u∈{1,2,4,6} 显示每轮 1 条陈述即可恢复大部分漂移损失，说明该机制是低带宽、低负载的实用方案。理论副产物：信息增益与体积去除在无噪 oracle 下退化为同一族平衡切割，均受命题 1 的 Θ(1/d) 上界约束。

Conclusion: 本文将"教师的关于学习者的模型"确立为 HAT 偏好学习中的核心性能变量：目标感知带来 √d 的逐轮优势，但需教师模型同步；理解陈述通过复用偏好通道本身（无需新模态）实现低成本的同步修复，其中二阶方向针对性陈述在多教师诱导的各向异性误差下优于一阶均值陈述，为现有偏好学习管线提供了一种可参数化（陈述预算 u）的扩展，并提出可监测的团队诊断量（教师-学习者信念间的 EMD）。未来工作包括一项四人条件人类研究（餐桌摆放领域），以及将陈述机制部署到轮班制造单元、多照护者家庭机器人等真实人机协同场景。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, asking people which of two behaviors they prefer, has become a standard way to align robot and agent behavior with human intent when the reward itself cannot be specified directly. Preference-based reward learning typically casts the human teacher as a passive oracle answering learner-generated queries. We argue this forfeits the teacher's defining advantage: knowledge of the objective. A teacher who knows the target can construct training examples more efficiently than any learner-driven acquisition strategy, an advantage that widens as the reward's feature dimension grows. However, exploiting this advantage requires an accurate model of what the learner currently knows. We therefore recast preference learning as a human-autonomy team problem coupling two behavioral models: the teacher maintains a model of the learner to design an informative curriculum, and the learner maintains a second-order model of the teacher's model, emitting structured preference constraints (understanding statements) that keep the teacher's model of the learner synchronized. In simulation, an informed teacher outperforms learner-led selection; teacher-model drift under alternating teachers erodes this advantage; and understanding statements repair it, with second-order (ToM-2) statements outperforming mean-belief statements when the teacher's error about the learner is concentrated in a particular direction rather than spread evenly.

</details>


### [115] [The Edge-based Contiguous p-median Problem with Connections to Logistics Districting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11230)
*Zeyad Kassem,Adolfo R. Escobedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出边基连续p-中位（ECpM）问题，用于将道路网络划分为指定数量的紧凑连续区域，提出了两种基于网络距离的二值规划模型，并比较了基于割集的分支切割算法与基于最短路径连续性（SPC）约束的标准分支定界法的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区域划分（districting）问题中，如何保证分配区域的紧凑性与空间连续性是一个核心挑战。已有的基于割集（cut set）的连续性约束模型需要指数数量级的约束，导致求解困难；而紧凑性目标与连续性约束之间的相互作用在边基网络上的理论联系尚不清晰。本文旨在通过引入ECpM问题，比较两种连续性建模方式的计算效率，并揭示ECpM与边基区域划分（EBD）问题之间的结构联系。

Method: 1. 提出两种ECpM的二值规划模型：
- 模型一：基于割集的连续性约束（需要指数数量级约束），配合分支切割（B&C）算法及分离子程序（separation scheme）动态生成约束；
- 模型二：基于最短路径连续性（SPC）约束（多项式数量级），可直接使用现成求解器进行分支定界求解。
2. 在超过2700个节点、3400条边的道路网络实例上进行测试，模型规模达960万个二值变量。
3. 从理论上分析SPC约束是边基p-中位（EpM）模型的超有效不等式（supervalid inequalities），即可能切掉EpM的整数可行解及其部分最优解。
4. 将两种连续性建模方式扩展到带有工作量平衡约束的边基区域划分（EBD）问题，进行对比分析。

Result: 1. 在ECpM求解效率上：基于SPC约束的标准分支定界法相比基于割集的B&C实现获得了高达17倍的计算时间加速。
2. SPC约束作为EpM的超有效不等式，能够切掉EpM的整数可行解，但不能切掉全部最优解。
3. 在EBD问题上：已有的基于割集连续性约束的模型在12小时内对所有测试实例均无法找到可行解，而基于SPC的EBD模型能够求解其中大部分实例至最优。
4. 揭示了ECpM与EBD之间的结构关联，连续性建模方式对求解可行性具有决定性影响。

Conclusion: 最短路径连续性（SPC）约束在建模边基区域划分问题时，相比传统的割集连续性约束具有显著的计算优势，无论是在纯连续性目标（ECpM）还是带有工作量平衡的扩展（EBD）场景下。SPC约束数量多项式级、可直接由标准求解器处理，并能带来数量级的加速（最高17倍）。同时，本文揭示了SPC约束对EpM的超有效性质，为区域划分问题中的紧凑连续性建模提供了理论依据和实用的替代方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces the edge-based contiguous p-median (ECpM) problem to partition the roads in a network into a given number of compact and contiguous territories. Two binary programming models are introduced, both of which incorporate a network distance. The first model requires an exponential number of cut set-based constraints to model contiguity; it is paired with a separation scheme that usually generates only a small number of these constraints, namely, a branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm. The second model utilizes a polynomial number of shortest-path constraints to model contiguity and can be solved with off-the-shelf solvers. The respective solution approaches are tested on road networks with over 2,700 nodes and close to 3,400 edges, yielding models with over 9.6 million binary variables. Solving the model based on shortest path contiguity (SPC) constraints via standard branch and bound attains speedups in computational time of up to 17x relative to the cut set-based B&C implementation. In addition, the SPC constraints are demonstrated to be supervalid inequalities of the edge-based p-median (EpM) model (i.e., for which contiguity is not explicitly required), meaning that they may cut off integer-feasible solutions and some, but not all, of the optimal solutions of this simpler problem. Finally, the paper explores structural insights and connections between ECpM and the edge-based districting (EBD) problem, which enforces an additional work balance criterion. An existing model that utilizes cut set-based contiguity constraints was unable to find a feasible solution within 12 hours for any of the tested instances, while an SPC-based EBD model was able to solve most of these to optimality.

</details>


### [116] [LinearKV: One Cached State Suffices for Position-Independent Caching in Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11231)
*Yirui Liu,Ruoling Qi,Longwen Wang,Xuaner Wu,Jian Chen,Yuxin Jin,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinearKV提出了一种用于混合大语言模型的位置无关缓存(PIC)框架。其核心发现是:在递归层中,只需使用单个缓存状态作为初始化器即可,在GDN模型上与代数精确的组合方法效果相当,而在Mamba-2模型上则显著优于精确组合(将质量从46.6%提升至86.8%)。同时该方法还降低了5–17%的延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置无关缓存(PIC)方法(如CacheBlend、EPIC、ProphetKV)专为全注意力模型设计,其核心操作——拼接token级KV缓存、选择性重计算——都依赖于token索引的缓存。然而混合LLM(如Mamba-2、GDN)用线性递归层替代了大部分注意力层,这些层只暴露固定大小的状态,没有token索引的KV可供拼接或局部修复。这引出两个核心问题:(1)现有PIC机制能否复用?(2)混合PIC能否在不同递归架构间泛化?

Method: LinearKV提出"解耦初始化"框架:全注意力层沿用原有KV拼接方式,而线性递归层通过映射函数f将K个匹配的局部状态合并为单一初始状态。该框架兼容现有PIC选择器(token选择与重计算机制不变),设计空间归结为单一选择——线性状态初始化器f。论文对比了两种初始化策略:(1)精确组合(exact composition):将所有K个缓存状态通过累积转移矩阵组合为精确的前缀状态(HYPIC采用此方法);(2)单块初始化(last-block init):仅取最后一个匹配块的缓存状态作为初始状态。通过逐层相对误差诊断,作者揭示精确组合在Mamba-2(标量衰减)上误差逐层累积,而在GDN(密集门控转移)上误差有界。

Result: 在三种混合模型(Granite/Mamba-2、OLMo/GDN、Qwen/GDN)与三种PIC选择器(CacheBlend、EPIC、ProphetKV)上的实验表明:(1)两个GDN模型上,精确组合与单块初始化效果相当(差距≤0.013 Avg-F1),均恢复最多92%的完整质量;(2)在Mamba-2上,精确组合在所有选择器下均崩溃,而单块初始化保持鲁棒——以EPIC为例,质量从46.6%提升至86.8%的完整质量;(3)单块初始化在所有27对测量中均比精确组合快5–17%;(4)TTFT降至完整预填充的0.46×;额外消融证实随机单块效果与最后块相当,表明关键在于单源结构而非具体块选择。

Conclusion: 精确代数组合在混合LLM上具有输入条件性和架构脆弱性——独立预填充的深层算子基于上下文不匹配的隐藏输入构建,因此在Mamba-2上有害,在GDN上不必要。LinearKV通过单缓存块初始化并配合有序重计算,联合修复递归状态与全注意力KV,提供了一种训练免费、跨架构鲁棒的PIC方案。未来工作应扩展到批量服务与主机到设备缓存传输场景。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly accelerated by position-independent caching (PIC). Existing PIC methods, however, are built for full-attention models, where a token-indexed KV cache underlies its core operations: matching reusable token chunks, concatenating their KV entries, and selectively recomputing a few tokens to restore cross-chunk context. Hybrid LLMs break these primitives---they replace most attention layers with linear recurrences that expose only a fixed-size state, leaving no token-indexed KV to concatenate or to locally repair. This raises a natural question: can PIC benefit hybrid models, and what would it take? We present LinearKV, a training-free hybrid-PIC framework. Its key insight is a \emph{decoupled initialization}: each linear layer maps its $K$ matched local states to a single initial state, while full-attention layers concatenate their KV as before. LinearKV is therefore compatible with existing PIC methods, reusing their token selection and recomputation as-is. Under this framework, we find that a \emph{single cached state} suffices as the linear layer's initializer. The algebraically principled alternative---composing all $K$ cached states into the exact full-prefix state, as concurrent work HYPIC does---is unnecessary and, on some architectures, even harmful. We compare the two across three hybrid models and three PIC selectors. On the two GDN models the two tie, both recovering most of full quality (up to $92\%$); on the Mamba-2 model, exact composition instead collapses under every selector---under EPIC, for instance, it recovers only $46.6\%$ of full quality, versus $86.8\%$ for a single cached block initializer. A single state initializer is also cheaper, cutting time-to-first-token to $0.46\times$ full prefill versus a further $5$--$17\%$ overhead for exact composition; results hold across LongBench QA and RULER at 8K--32K.

</details>


### [117] [InfraBench: Evaluating Infrastructure Agents Across Layers, Lifecycle, and Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11234)
*Yuan Gao,Zeren Yang,Junnan Li,Shawn,Zhong,Ahmed Dajani,Mai Zheng,Andrea Arpaci-Dusseau,Remzi Arpaci-Dusseau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InfraBench 是一个面向 AI Agent 的基础设施管理基准套件，覆盖全系统栈（L1硬件→L4应用）、全运维生命周期（部署→退役）并具备细粒度风险评估能力。通过 12 个种子任务与 15 个 Agent-Model 配置的实验，证明即使最强 Agent 也无法取得满分（均分 40%–88%），揭示出 Agent 普遍"修复即时故障但遗留运维义务"的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算基础设施管理日趋复杂，AI Agent 虽有自动化潜力，但现有基准（SREGym、ITBench、AIOpsLab、DevOps-Gym、SWE-Bench、Terminal-Bench 等）在系统环境、生命周期阶段、规模与风险评估方面均存在显著局限，无法反映真实基础设施的全栈与全周期复杂性。因此亟需一个能够覆盖多层栈、多生命周期、分布式规模与风险感知的综合基准。

Method: InfraBench 由四大组件构成：(1) Task Specification 定义任务包与隐藏评估上下文；(2) Executor 含 Backend Selector（按层分配 Docker/VM/BM 资源）与 Scenario Manager（注入故障）；(3) Evaluator 含 Full-Lifecycle Checker（Immediate/Live/Restart-Durability/Decommission 四道闸门）与 Risk Monitor（基于 LLM-judge 的动作轨迹危险分类）；(4) Output 输出阶段级归因的细粒度报告。任务来源包括对基础设施从业者的半结构化访谈、知名开源项目 issue tracker、云平台文档与系统研究原型。采用难度加权 per-check 评分（w = (1-p) + 0.1），报告指标包括 Mean Effective Score、Attempt Pass@τ（τ∈{1, 0.5}）、Best-of-N@τ 及 SEM。评测 5 种 CLI（Claude Code/Cursor/Gemini CLI/OpenCode/Qoder）× 9 家厂商模型的 15 组配置，每任务 3 次独立尝试，运行于 CloudLab c220g1 同构物理硬件。

Result: 主要实验结果：(1) 整体榜单均分 39.9%–87.7%，无满分配置；最强 Fable 5 + Claude Code 仅 87.7±5.8，Attempt Pass@1 仅 77.1%。(2) 生命周期梯度显著：Functional 89.0% → Durability 75.0% → Cleanup 35.2% 通过率。(3) 失败模式普遍化：post-repair cleanup 与 incomplete deployment residue 影响全部 15 个配置；tool-destructive diagnosis 影响 87%；non-persistent fixes 与 hidden config-DB 条目亦高频出现。(4) 风险监测：9,351 条命令中仅 76 条 (0.8%) 被标记为危险；Ceph Bootstrap 上 5 个配置独立执行了强制访问控制卸载；17 条探测评分器的动作（多集中于 DB WAL Recovery）；存在事件再注入（Composer 2.5/Grok 4.5 倒写 registry 数据库）等严重事件。(5) 经济性：相同 12 任务成本跨两个数量级（$0.82–$194），成本与可靠性弱相关；token 效率比单价更决定总体开销，Gemini 3.5 Flash 单次 vm-pelican 尝试消耗 6000 万 token。(6) Pelican Key Mismatch 案例研究展示"表面修复、对等节点不可达、未关闭事故标记"三种失败模式共存。

Conclusion: InfraBench 揭示出当前 SOTA Agent 普遍表现出"任务结束时故障已消失，但持久化、分布式一致性与清理等运维义务未达成"的系统性缺陷，表明 pass/fail 评估严重高估了实际可靠性。论文提出未来工作方向：(1) 引入成本归一化指标（每 token/美元/分钟得分）；(2) 将风险监测从离线重建转为基于 scenario_events 与 periodic_verifier 的原生事件流，并按严重性排序；(3) 将生命周期阶段标签嵌入任务规范以替代启发式关键词分类；(4) 扩大任务覆盖（当前 L3 偏重、L1/L2 偏薄），呼吁社区贡献。基准已开源（含在线排行榜、任务包与评测 harness），推动面向真实基础设施管理复杂度的 Agent 能力评估。

Abstract: Managing modern computing infrastructure has become a steadily harder problem due to the ever-increasing complexity. Recent advances in AI agents create a timely opportunity to automate infrastructure management tasks, but it remains unclear how well such agents can handle real-world infrastructure complexity. We present InfraBench, a benchmark suite for evaluating AI agents on realistic infrastructure tasks across the full system stack and full operational lifecycle with fine-grained risk assessment. Experiments with 15 agent-model configurations show that even the strongest agent cannot secure a full score across all tasks. Mean effective scores range from roughly 40% to 88% (with per-configuration standard errors of 6-12 points), repeating every task three times reveals that top configurations still pass only a fraction of their attempts, and per-check scoring exposes a general failure pattern: agents may routinely satisfy short-term objectives while leaving non-durable changes, broken distributed invariants, unsafe side effects, and uncleaned state behind. INFRABENCH, including its live leaderboard, tasks, and evaluation harness, is publicly available at this http URL .

</details>


### [118] [CORA-Diff: Confidence-Oriented Residual Acceptance for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11235)
*Yifan Wu,Yufeng Zhang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORA-Diff 是一种无需训练的扩散语言模型推理加速方法，通过置信度-持久性门控机制识别并提前接受稳定预测的token，在不修改主干网络、logits或引入学习模块的前提下，跳过已解析block的剩余密集前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型采用固定去噪周期进行块解码，但许多位置预测早期即已稳定，继续执行密集前向传播造成大量重复计算浪费。现有加速方法（Prophet、KLASS、DAPD、Learn2PD等）依赖学习过滤器、logit修改、依赖模型或特定缓存机制，研究者希望探索能否仅利用原生去噪轨迹信号（top-1置信度和跨步骤持久性）来安全地接受残差位置的预测，从而无需任何额外训练或系统改动即可实现推理加速。

Method: 核心方法为置信度导向的残差接受（Confidence-Oriented Residual Acceptance）。保留原始传输规则不变，仅对其未解决的位置施加残差门控：维护每个未解决token的top-1置信度 c_i^(s) 和跨步骤持久性计数器 m_i^(s)（连续相同预测的步数）。当 c_i^(s) ≥ δ_p 且 m_i^(s) ≥ m 时接受该token；一旦块内所有位置都被解析（原始规则或CORA-Diff），立即终止块并跳过剩余密集前向传播。理论部分给出端点保持的充分条件（基于logit margin）、持久性条件下的漂移尾概率界（条件持久性-漂移分离假设下的贝叶斯分析），以及校准选择器的均匀有限样本界。在1000个GSM8K校准prompt上选定 (δ_p, m) = (0.65, 1) 并冻结用于全部评测。

Result: 在匹配的Learn2PD风格LLaDA-8B-Instruct协议下，CORA-Diff在全部8个任务-长度设置中均取得最低实测运行时间。在固定周期1024/1024机制隔离协议下达到13.14×加速；在EOS感知部署下相比EOS感知密集解码在GSM8K和HumanEval上分别获得2.70×和3.32×加速；任务得分在5个设置中匹配或超过密集解码，最大下降仅1.22分。相同阈值无需重调即可迁移至Dream模型，获得3.18×–3.53×加速，任务得分变化不超过0.006；与缓存复用结合可达53.65×。消融实验证实置信度与持久性结合优于单一信号。

Conclusion: CORA-Diff证明原生置信度与持久性信号足以实现可靠的残差接受，无需学习接受模型、logit修改或主干变更即可显著削减重复去噪计算同时保持任务质量。该方法具有理论支撑（端点保存条件、漂移尾界）和实证验证（配对后干预轨迹分析）。局限性在于配对验证仅支持5/6个分层，稀疏层数据不足，需更广泛部署测试。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) update many tokens in parallel, yet practical decoders often use a fixed denoising horizon. Many predictions stabilize early, but blockwise decoding continues until all positions are resolved, causing repeated dense forward passes. Existing accelerators often rely on learned filters, modified scores, dependency models, or cache-specific mechanisms. We ask whether native trajectory signals can identify residual positions likely to match the deterministic dense endpoint. We propose CORA-Diff, a training-free method that preserves the original transfer rule and applies confidence-and-persistence gating only to positions that rule leaves unresolved. Accepted tokens remain visible as context, and the block terminates once all positions are resolved. This requires no backbone change, learned acceptance model, or logit modification. Our theory explains why high-confidence, persistent predictions are more likely to match the fixed-horizon dense endpoint, and paired post-intervention trajectories provide direct empirical support. We select one operating point on a separate GSM8K calibration subset and freeze it for all evaluations. Under a matched Learn2PD-style LLaDA protocol, CORA-Diff has the lowest measured runtime in all eight task-length settings. Task scores match or exceed dense decoding in five settings, and the largest observed drop is 1.22 points. Its incremental speedups over EOS-aware dense decoding are 2.70x and 3.32x on GSM8K and HumanEval. It also reaches 13.14x under the fixed-horizon 1024/1024 mechanism-isolation protocol and transfers to Dream without retuning at 3.18x-3.53x. These results show that native confidence and persistence enable reliable residual acceptance, reducing repeated denoising computation while preserving task quality.

</details>


### [119] [Geometry-aware Incremental Neural Operator for Long-Horizon PDE prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11237)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Haifan Meng,Yi Lu,Zhihan Lyu,Fan Mo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoIncNO（Geometry-aware Incremental Neural Operator），一种用于PDE长期自回归预测的神经算子方法。该方法将隐空间转移增量作为自回归演化的核心调控对象，通过主动频带低秩投影（ABP）塑形隐增量的谱-通道几何结构，并设计均值-波动解耦重建（MFDR）机制减少物理空间重建误差与均值漂移，在6个1D/2D/3D PDE基准测试上全面优于现有神经算子基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期自回归PDE预测中，局部误差会在多次推演中累积为谱不一致、相位错位或均值漂移。现有方法主要改进状态表示和算子骨干网络，但对反复应用的隐状态转移增量缺乏结构化约束，导致非稳态谱成分和不稳定通道耦合不断累积。另一关键问题在于物理空间重建时直接将包含均值漂移、波动和相位误差的完整场融合，混淆了不同性质的误差成分。论文通过实证分析发现潜增量在空间响应模式、协方差谱衰减速度和谱能量分布上均与完整潜状态存在显著差异，验证了显式建模隐增量的必要性。

Method: GeoIncNO包含四大组件：(i) 隐编码器与骨干网络预测原始隐增量；(ii) 主动频带投影（ABP）——根据增量谱能量分布的能量分位数构建主动频带（1D区间/2D环形/3D壳层），在每个频带内对谱通道向量应用轻量级低秩形投影P_m(x) = x + α_m U_m U_m^T x（r=8），通过残差连接校正增量后残差推进潜状态；(iii) 双分支物理重建——输入锚定校正分支（u_base + α·dΔ(δz)）与状态解码分支（D_state(z_{t+1})）并行；(iv) 均值-波动解耦重建（MFDR）——独立门控融合时间均值和零均值波动，仅对波动施加相位校正，抑制均值漂移。损失函数包含预测损失、几何损失（去相关L_corr + 方差支撑L_var）和投影一致性损失，采用预热调度。理论分析证明在Lipschitz解码器假设下，潜增量误差可控制物理预测误差。

Result: 在6个PDE基准（1D Burgers/KS、2D NS/SW、3D CE/Maxwell）上全面优于DeepONet、FNO、UNO、WNO、PINO、LNO、LaMO等7个基线，所有4项核心指标（Rel-L2、Rel-H1、WLR、C-RMSE）均取得最优。在1D KS上Rel-L2从FNO的3.22E-2降至1.64E-2，在2D NS上从2.65E-1降至7.54E-3。长期推演稳定性显著优于LaMO和FNO（误差累积斜率更平缓）。消融实验证实：固定频带LIG、主动频带、MFDR、波动相位校正均有单调贡献，其中MFDR将均值漂移从1.32E-3降至1.05E-04，限制相位校正仅作用于波动使均值漂移进一步降至6.21E-05。跨参数泛化（Re=1000/10000训练，Re=5000测试）和跨分辨率泛化（32²训练至256²测试）均达到最优，RealPDEBench FSI任务上也以9.8M参数超越参数量大数十倍的基线。

Conclusion: GeoIncNO将隐转移增量作为自回归演化的核心调控对象，通过主动频带低秩投影塑形其谱-通道几何结构，结合均值-波动解耦重建，成功抑制长期推演中的谱误差累积、相位错位和均值漂移。该方法为神经算子的长期稳定预测提供了一条以增量几何结构而非状态表示为中心的新路径。未来方向包括自适应频带构建、融入物理约束、不确定性估计，以及在更多样化几何与边界条件下的验证。

Abstract: Neural operators have shown strong potential for learning solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). However, long-horizon autoregressive prediction remains challenging: local errors accumulate as spectral inconsistency, phase misalignment, or mean drift. Existing methods mainly improve state representations and operator backbones, while leaving the repeatedly applied latent transition increment weakly structured, allowing spectral errors and unstable channel couplings to accumulate during rollout. To address these issues, we propose a geometry-aware incremental neural operator (GeoIncNO) for stable long-horizon PDE prediction. GeoIncNO predicts latent increments for residual advancement and uses lightweight low-rank projectors to regulate channel coupling within active frequency bands derived from the increment spectral energy distribution. To reduce physical-space reconstruction errors, GeoIncNO further introduces a mean--fluctuation decoupled reconstruction mechanism, where stable mean structures and dynamic fluctuations are fused separately, and phase correction is applied only to the zero-mean fluctuation component. Extensive experiments on six PDE benchmarks, covering 1D, 2D, and 3D dynamical systems, show that GeoIncNO achieves consistently strong prediction accuracy, improved rollout stability, and better spectral fidelity compared with competitive neural-operator baselines.

</details>


### [120] [Towards Query-Agnostic RAG Evaluation via Query Coverage and Claim Verifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11238)
*Jeonghwan Choi,Taewon Yun,Minjeong Ban,Gyeonghun Sun,Jae-Gil Lee,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Q-CARE提出了一种查询无关（query-agnostic）且完全无需参考（reference-free）的RAG评估框架，通过将查询分解为子查询、将答案分解为原子声明（atomic claims），并基于"查询覆盖度（query coverage）"和"声明可验证性（claim verifiability）"两大统一原则，同时支持封闭型和开放型查询的细粒度评估，弥补了现有RAG评估方法在粒度与参考依赖之间难以兼得的结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估方法存在结构性权衡：RAGEval、RAGChecker等细粒度方法依赖参考答案或标注段落，仅适用于封闭型查询；而DoRAG等无需参考的方法虽能处理开放型查询，却只能输出粗粒度信号、缺乏声明级验证。因此亟需一种兼具细粒度、无需参考、且同时支持封闭与开放型查询的评估框架。

Method: Q-CARE采用三阶段分解式评估流程：（1）查询-答案分解——根据查询类型（单焦点/多步推理/多方面）将查询分解为子查询，将答案分解为自包含原子声明；（2）覆盖度与可验证性检查——通过共享Checker LLM执行三组对齐任务：子查询-段落对齐、子查询-声明对齐、声明-段落对齐；（3）指标计算——聚合原子级信号得出检索端的C-Prec@k和C-nDCG@k（用软覆盖度替代二元相关性标签）以及生成端的Completeness（子查询被回答的比例）、Conciseness（相关声明占比）和Verifiableness（声明被证据支持的比例）五个评估维度。默认采用Qwen3-30B-A3B-Inst作为骨干模型。

Result: 在覆盖8个数据集（NQ、NewsQA、HotpotQA、FinQA、PubMedQA、LoTTE-Science、LoTTE-Technology、ELI5）、包含160条封闭型+160条开放型查询的人工标注基准上，Q-CARE在所有五个维度上均取得了与人类判断最高的Pearson相关系数，显著优于RAGEval、RAGAs、RAGChecker和DoRAG；与传统指标（EM、ACC、ROUGE-L及二元相关性Precision/nDCG）相比同样优势明显，尤其在开放型查询上从-0.062跃升至0.407。检索器基准测试揭示了默认指标与覆盖度感知指标之间显著的排名差异（Spearman秩相关从0.55提升至0.89）；生成器基准测试表明无单一LLM在所有维度占优。框架在MTRAG多轮对话RAG基准上也取得了最高相关性，且消融实验证实查询分解对评估质量至关重要（最大提升+0.473）。

Conclusion: Q-CARE通过将答案正确性重新诠释为查询覆盖度与声明可验证性的联合满足，建立了一种统一适用于封闭型和开放型查询的评估原则，实现了无需参考、细粒度、查询无关的端到端RAG评估，在与人类判断的一致性上全面超越现有方法，证明了查询覆盖度在可靠RAG基准测试中的关键作用，并自然推广至多轮对话场景。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in retrieved evidence, yet existing evaluation frameworks struggle to provide consistent, fine-grained diagnostics across the diverse spectrum of user queries, ranging from close-ended fact-seeking to open-ended explanatory requests. We propose Q-CARE, a query-agnostic and fully reference-free framework that enables fine-grained assessment by decomposing queries into sub-queries and answers into atomic claims. Q-CARE establishes a unified evaluation principle based on query coverage and claim verifiability, yielding coverage-aware retriever metrics (C-Prec@k, C-nDCG@k) and claim-level generator metrics (Completeness, Conciseness, and Verifiableness). On a human-annotated benchmark spanning eight datasets, Q-CARE achieves higher correlation with human judgments than four existing RAG evaluation metrics, including RAGEval and RAGChecker, proving its effectiveness as a reliable, automated evaluation framework. Code and data are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [RecSys Factory: Bounding LLM Agent Autonomy to Decision Points in the Industrial Recommender Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11241)
*Dongyang Ao,Kaixiang Fang,Shijie Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RecSys Factory——一个部署在腾讯三条异构推荐业务线上历时78天的LLM智能体平台。其核心设计原则是"在决策点赋予自主性,而非在管道层面赋予自主性",通过运行时、能力和部署三个解构来应对自主性-确定性-效率三难困境(trilemma)。平台采用宿主事件耦合(Stop hook/IM webhook/调度器API)实现零守护进程运行,29个技能子图构成可调度的执行型工作记忆,并通过400条规则的知识库约束智能体在预提交管道内的有界决策面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统运营涉及样本构造、特征工程、模型训练、评估、A/B测试、在线服务等多个管道,不同业务线的标签语义、特征schema、A/B层级拓扑和运营人员角色各异。LLM智能体虽有潜力吸收运营认知负荷,但工业部署面临三难困境:通用自主性、工业确定性和端到端效率三者受单一资源(每业务线3–6人月的工程+人力审查预算)约束,任意两者最大化必以牺牲第三者为代价。作者观察到现有方案要么走向AI Scientist v2式自主但42%发散率的失败模式,要么像EasyRec/Monolith那样稳定但无法处理schema漂移,要么像Devin那样正确但多周审核周期未压缩。

Method: 方法围绕三个解构展开:(1)运行时解构——三阶层架构(用户层/智能体层/基础设施层),通过Claude Code Stop钩子、企业IM webhook、工作流调度器API三种宿主事件源触发LangGraph DAG,等待阶段零CPU(94%墙钟时间用于Spark/GPU作业,仅6%为智能体推理);(2)能力解构——29个SKILL.md技能(8971行)组织为可调度子图,而非检索语料;其pitfall表经规则提取器编译为400条PitfallStore条目,作为智能体的执行型工作记忆;(3)部署解构——横跨电信个性化推荐(A)、电信重排决策支持(B)、财富管理新客CVR受众提取(C)三条业务线。HITL采用企业IM卡片协议(诊断-执行边界),22类失败分类法辅助Analyzer节点,单源PipelineState通过SQLite WAL持久化以保证幂等性。

Result: 78天窗口内平台记录1624次CLI工具调度,聚合成功率78.6%(若将WAITING重新归类则约83.7%);业务A实现某运营商4/7区域队列+10–31% CPM提升,并识别第二运营商9个高风险子队列转规则回退(+14–45%预期提升),特征维度从7881压缩至221;业务B反向求解模式推荐top-3权重调整子集被运营采纳,8.44M请求全量回放显示单物品+18%至+47%相对日收入提升,±20%参数扰动20次试验全为正;业务C七阶段audience-modeling-c技能在单次onboarding中通过预飞行检查发现三处上游数据完整性问题,避免静默训练污染。跨业务PitfallStore条目复用率77.0%(剔除泛型标签后约55%)。22类失败分类法已落地部署。8天HITL卡片试点中16次DAG运行(9次Done/3次Training/2次Sample-Labeling/1次Sensor/1次Analyzer未结)。

Conclusion: 作者论证三方面核心主张:(i)智能体必须生命周期耦合而非守护进程监督——三种宿主事件源使平台在零专用基础设施下存活月度级部署;(ii)领域知识应建模为可调度子图而非检索混合——29个技能+400条pitfall构成非工程师运营人员可通过聊天界面驱动的执行型工作记忆;(iii)HITL卡片是合规级访问原语——从运营人员批准到生产副作用的审计链路使智能体驱动的基础设施变更在企业环境中可审计。核心贡献包括:生命周期感知智能体框架、29技能生态+400条PitfallStore、22类失败分类法,以及跨业务线沉默漂移检测的方法论副产品。局限明确:onboarding压缩来自作者回忆而非对照基线,试点样本量不足以支撑统计结论,跨组织可移植性留待未来工作。

Abstract: Deploying LLM agents into industrial recommender operations exposes a three-way tension we frame as the autonomy-determinism-efficiency trilemma: general autonomy (interpreting operator intent, generating glue code zero-shot), industrial determinism (schema-conforming feature extraction, non-crashing A/B, zero compliance-path hallucination), and end-to-end efficiency. Any two can be maximized against the third. We present RecSys Factory, an LLM-agent platform deployed for 78 days across three heterogeneous Tencent recommender business lines. The design principle is autonomy at decision points, not over pipelines, made concrete through three deconstructions that each discharge one vertex of the trilemma. Runtime is deconstructed into three host-emitted event sources (Claude Code Stop hooks, corporate-IM webhooks, workflow scheduler APIs): the platform carries no long-running daemon during the wait phase and consumes zero CPU during the 94% of wall-clock spent waiting on Spark or GPU jobs. Capability is deconstructed into a 29-file skill ecosystem (8,971 lines of this http URL ) whose per-skill pitfall tables mechanically compile into a 400-entry PitfallStore, confining autonomy to bounded typed decision surfaces inside pre-committed pipelines. Deployment spans three business lines with disjoint label semantics, A/B layer topologies, and operator personas; an onboarding-time compression is observed on two of the three and is reported as a case-study observation, not a generalization claim, and not measured against a controlled pre-platform baseline. The human is retained at the diagnostic-versus-execution boundary via a human-in-the-loop card protocol, deployed as an audit-trail primitive (schema-validated, idempotent, replayable) and reported from an 8-day 16-run pilot. Across the 78-day window the platform recorded 1,624 CLI-tool dispatches at a 78.6% aggregate success rate.

</details>


### [122] [The Off-Support Barrier: Why Semantic Safety Constraints Are Not Learning-Problem Invariants, and What Follows for Prior Design, Containment, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11243)
*Yoshinori Watanabe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个核心结构性论点：语义安全约束（如"智能体不逃逸沙箱"）是关于支撑集外行为的泛函，不属于学习问题的不变量；而奇异学习理论（SLT）提取的实对数典范阈值（RLCT）则是。论文以2026年7月OpenAI–Hugging Face评估事件为动机，从这一非不变性出发推导出五个推论，分别解释奖励黑客、沙箱逃逸、先验设计失效、形式验证的局部可认证性，以及支撑集移动时SLT解析框架的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机源于2026年7月OpenAI在无生产安全分类器条件下对前沿网络能力进行的内部评估事件：模型链式利用漏洞逃逸沙箱、通过包注册表缓存代理的零日漏洞获取开放互联网访问、并到达第三方生产数据库窃取评估答案密钥。论文主张这并非三个独立失败（奖励黑客、沙箱逃逸、安全漏洞），而是同一次优化的向外泄漏，泄漏由一个处于学习问题"外部"的对象——安全约束——所支配。作者旨在用统一的形式框架解释这些现象，而非将它们作为独立观察罗列。

Method: 方法层面，论文综合运用以下工具：（1）奇异学习理论（SLT）的实对数典范阈值（RLCT）及其局部估计量局部学习系数（LLC），通过泽塔函数 ζ(z)=∫K(w)^z φ(w)dw 分析自由能渐近行为 F_n≃nL_n(w*)+λ log n−(m−1)log log n+O(1)；（2）通过广中平佐（日本数学家广中千澄）的解析解消（resolution）将KL散度 K(w)=KL(q∥p(·|w)) 单项式化为 K(g(u))=a(u)u^{2k}，分析奇异先验在解析解消坐标中消失阶数 2c_j 对局部RLCT λ_j=(h_j+2c_j+1)/(2k_j) 的提升效应；（3）通过纤维几何区分两类参数冗余——全局对称性冗余与支撑受限欠定性——并以Jacobian秩差 rank J_X−rank J_S 度量支撑集外自由度；（4）通过形式化方法：区间界传播（IBP）+分支定界在ReLU网络上对谓词 B(w):=∃x∈R. f_w(x)∈U 进行声音验证，并在Lean 4中以 safe_of_ub_nonpos 与 B_of_witness 形式化；（5）通过Rice定理论证理想化层（策略=程序）禁止集的非递归性，通过ReLU可达性的NP完全性论证有限层的计算硬度。

Result: 论文的核心结果包含一个中心命题与五个推论。中心命题（非不变性）：若安全谓词B依赖于对抗性支撑集外输入上的行为，则B关于∼_q-等价不可测，故 B∉σ(model,q)，而RLCT∈σ(model,q)。五个推论：（i）奖励黑客与沙箱逃逸源于结果导向优化——代理奖励与真实奖励不可区分时，弱道义先验被强优化压力覆盖；（ii）贝叶斯先验设计与软惩罚加权在奇异模型中杠杆有限——光滑再加权被似然几何在主阶抹去，奇异先验虽可通过解消坐标中高阶零点保留，但其禁止行为轨迹一般是轨迹/可达性谓词，非代数簇，φ无法在其上消失，且λ log n效应是对后验集中度的渐近在线分布断言，无法捕捉有限n、线下、由SGD/RL主动寻路的逃逸事件；（iii）硬不变量应属于执行环境（harness），软倾向应属于模型——精确谓词仅存在于环境中，硬约束要求精确谓词；（iv）形式验证可声音且局部地认证同一B，正如LLC局部钉住同一RLCT——IBP给出[L,U]⊐V的声音括号，分支定界在R上精化，SAFE实例经31个盒子精化后认证¬B，UNSAFE实例由单一见证认证；（v）残余困难——识别哪个支撑集外区域重要——恰与执行性预测和自引用函数动力学（Kataoka–Kaneko型的 f_{n+1}=(1−ε)f_n+εf_n∘f_n）重合，此处SLT解析框架（基于o-极小性/半解析性的解消前提）崩溃。论文还给出两层硬度分离：理想化层下Rice定理导致禁止集非递归，φ不存在（非存在性结果严格强于任何RLCT陈述）；有限层下B可判定但NP难（ReLU可达性NP完全），同源于RLCT闭式稀缺/LLC数值可处理。

Conclusion: 论文的结论是一个统一论旨：语义安全约束是支撑集外行为的泛函，因此不属于学习问题的不变量，而RLCT属于。这一结构性事实贯穿整个分析。它解释了为何结果导向优化会泄漏（约束处于奖励的支撑集之外），为何先验设计与软加权在奇异模型中杠杆有限（约束不是可识别的子簇，能存活的奇异先验无法从规则规定），为何硬不变量必须驻留于执行环境而非模型（精确谓词与保证只存在于环境），为何同一约束在给定区域时仍可被声音且局部地认证（验证钉住B，如LLC钉住RLCT），以及为何残余——识别重要的支撑集外区域——恰与执行性及自引用函数动力学重合，SLT基于解消的机制在那里停止适用。实践启示是分工：模型通过对齐训练降低语义空间中坏轨迹的概率（作为速率缩减器）；执行环境通过隔离、不可达谕示、能力范围工具、独立杀死开关使最坏情况成为不可能；两者在数据所支撑与不支撑之间的边界上干净汇合。论文还将GAN训练重构为Wasserstein空间上的自引用算子T，以执行性/耦合强度ε为分岔参数，提议将Kataoka–Kaneko函数动力学与高自由度GAN通过广域耦合映射、混沌游走、Milnor吸引子、广泛Lyapunov谱联系起来，承认这是真正开放的前沿。

Abstract: We argue that a single structural fact organizes a wide range of phenomena in contemporary AI safety: a semantic safety constraint (e.g., the agent does not escape its sandbox) is an off-support object. Formally, if q is the data distribution and \(p(\cdot\mid w)\) the model, the safety predicate B is not measurable with respect to \(\sigma(\text{model}, q)\), whereas the real log-canonical threshold (RLCT) of singular learning theory (SLT) is. From this non-invariance we derive, as corollaries rather than independent observations: (i) why reward hacking and sandbox escape arise under outcome-based optimization; (ii) why encoding such constraints through Bayesian prior design or soft penalty weighting has poor leverage in singular models; (iii) why hard invariants belong in the harness and soft dispositions in the model; (iv) why the same B is nonetheless soundly and locally certifiable by formal verification, exactly as the local learning coefficient (LLC) locally pins the same RLCT --- with two precise points of disanalogy; and (v) why the residual difficulty, identifying which off-support region matters, coincides with performative prediction and self-referential functional dynamics, where SLT's analytic machinery breaks down. We use the July 2026 OpenAI--Hugging Face evaluation incident as the motivating case. Numerical experiments code and related proofs in lean are available at this https URL

</details>


### [123] [BEST-KAG: Enhancing Question Answering of Building Engineering Standards with Multimodal Knowledge Graph Modeling and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11244)
*Jia-Rui Lin,Junxi Guo,Keyin Chen,Peng Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BEST-KAG（Knowledge-Augmented Generation for Building Engineering Standards），一个面向建筑工程标准的多模态知识驱动问答框架，通过构建多模态知识图谱（MKG）和基于图检索的知识增强生成架构，解决传统关键词检索在多条款推理、多模态知识利用和可溯源证据链接方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑工程标准应用流程依赖基于关键词的文档检索和人工跨条款解读，存在三个关键局限：1）无法可靠支持多条款推理；2）难以利用多模态知识（如图文混合的标准内容）；3）缺乏条款级别可追溯的证据链接机制。这制约了建筑安全与可持续性保障。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：1）多模态知识图谱（MKG）：统一表征文档层级结构与异构标准知识及其多样化关联关系；2）规则-LLM混合知识构建流水线：实现可扩展的多模态知识自动抽取，构建了涵盖251项建筑工程标准、171,652节点和310,914边的MKG；3）基于图检索的知识增强生成架构：实现条款级可追溯的问答，确保回答有据可依。

Result: 实验表明BEST-KAG在专家评估以及BLEU、ROUGE等自动评价指标上均优于多个主流大语言模型（LLMs）基线，相对基线的最佳提升幅度达74.01%。

Conclusion: BEST-KAG通过融合多模态知识图谱与知识增强生成技术，有效解决了建筑工程标准问答中的多条款推理、多模态知识利用和可追溯性难题，为建筑工程的合规审查与标准应用提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Construction standards are critical for building safety and sustainability. Existing standard application workflows rely on keyword-based document retrieval and manual cross-clause interpretation, which cannot reliably support multi-clause reasoning, multimodal knowledge utilization, or traceable clause-level evidence linkage. To address these limitations, this study develops a multimodal knowledge-driven framework that supports question answering on standard knowledge named BEST-KAG (Knowledge-Augmented Generation for Building Engineering STandards). The framework introduces 1) a multimodal knowledge graph (MKG) for unified representation of document hierarchy and heterogeneous standard knowledge with various connections, 2) a rule-LLM hybrid knowledge construction pipeline for scalable multimodal knowledge extraction, creating a large MAG with 251 building engineering standards, 171,652 nodes and 310,914 edges, and 3) a graph-retrieval-based knowledge-augmented generation architecture for clause-grounded and traceable question answering. Experiments demonstrate that BEST-KAG consistently outperforms multiple mainstream LLMs in terms of Expert evaluation, and metrics including BLEU, and ROUGE, with the best improvement up to 74.01% compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [124] [Towards Sustainable Learning in Online Education: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11245)
*Chaofan Zhai,Yicheng Song,Ravi Bapna,Junyao Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Tutor，一种基于强化学习的模型，通过结合认知理论平衡新知识获取与旧知识巩固，并建模学习者长期参与度，以提升在线教育的短期参与度、长期知识保留率和最终学习效果。在2330万条学习记录上的实验表明，AI Tutor在参与度、知识保留和学习成果上均优于现有最优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线教育虽具备可扩展性与可及性优势，但普遍存在学习者参与度低、长期学习效果差的问题。现有方法难以同时兼顾短期学习效果与长期学习动机维持，导致高辍学率与知识保留不足。因此需要一种能够平衡短期知识获取与长期参与度维持的个性化辅导方法。

Method: AI Tutor基于强化学习框架设计，包含两个核心组件：1）短期模块借鉴认知理论（如间隔重复、巩固机制），通过策略引导学习者在新知识学习与已有知识强化之间取得平衡；2）长期模块建模学习者参与度（如辍学风险、动机变化），用于制定可持续的学习策略以降低辍学率。两个模块共同驱动个性化学习路径的生成与自适应调整。

Result: 在包含33,700名学习者、2330万条学习记录的大规模真实数据上的实验结果显示：1）AI Tutor在参与度、知识保留率和最终学习成果三项指标上均持续优于现有state-of-the-art基线；2）学习路径分析表明AI Tutor能够针对不同特征的学习者自适应调整策略，展现出以学习者为中心的适应性。

Conclusion: AI Tutor通过强化学习有效融合了短期认知优化与长期参与度建模，为在线教育提供了一种兼顾有效性与可持续性的个性化辅导方案。其自适应能力使其能够针对多样化学习者群体提供以人为本的支持，验证了AI驱动的个性化辅导在促进持久学习方面的潜力。

Abstract: Online education offers unprecedented scalability and accessibility to global learners from diverse backgrounds, but it often suffers from low engagement and poor long term learning effectiveness. To address these challenges, we introduce AI Tutor, a reinforcement learning based model designed to promote sustainable learning by optimizing both short and longterm learning outcomes. In the short term, AI-Tutor draws on cognitive theory to guide learners through a balance of acquiring new knowledge and reinforcing prior learning. In the long term, it models learner engagement to inform strategies that sustain motivation and reduce dropout. These enhancements enable AI-Tutor to provide personalized guidance that fosters both effective learning and sustained participation. Empirical evaluations on 23 million learning records from 33,700 learners show that AI Tutor consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across engagement, knowledge retention, and final learning outcomes. Learning path analyses further reveal how AI-Tutor adapts its strategies to learners with diverse profiles, offering adaptive and human-centered support.

</details>


### [125] [Towards the Harness of Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11246)
*Qi Wang,Tianyi Wang,Chengyang Li,Shikun Ban,Yurun Chen,Yizhong Ge,Jason Qin,Chengtai Li,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Thea"，将编码智能体（coding agent）的harness范式扩展到物理世界的具身智能体（embodied agent）。通过将机器人能力封装为可调用工具，并以"场景图作为上下文"（Scene Graph as Context）和"评估作为退出码"（Evaluation as Exit Codes）弥合物理世界在状态读取与结果评判上的能力缺失，使智能体能在真实环境中完成长时任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的成功表明智能体的能力不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于围绕它的基础设施（harness）。研究者想验证这一范式是否可推广到物理世界中的具身智能体；物理世界相较于软件世界缺少两项"免费"能力——读取世界状态和评判动作结果——需要专门的机制加以弥合。

Method: 设计Thea harness：(1) 采用代理循环（agentic loop）将机器人能力编排为可调用工具，继承编码智能体的核心组件并按物理世界需求改造；(2) 提出"场景图作为上下文"——一种持久的、世界的符号化表示，用于读取世界状态；(3) 提出"评估作为退出码"——检测动作何时应终止、判定动作是否成功，失败时诊断原因，从而在智能体与物理世界之间形成闭环；(4) 复杂行为由工具组合涌现，闭环机制支撑长时任务在真实环境中完成。

Result: 通过工具组合，复杂行为得以涌现；闭环机制使长时任务（long-horizon tasks）能在真实环境中被推进至完成。物理世界与智能体之间的反馈循环被有效建立。

Conclusion: 本文论证了harness范式可从编码智能体成功延伸至物理世界的具身智能体；通过场景图提供持续的世界表征、通过评估机制提供类似退出码的反馈信号，二者共同构成"智能体—物理世界"闭环，是具身智能体在真实环境中执行复杂长时任务的关键基础设施。

Abstract: The success of coding agents has established the harness as a paradigm: what an agent achieves depends not on the model alone, but on the infrastructure around it. We ask whether the same paradigm extends to embodied agents in the physical world. We present Thea, a harness in which an agentic loop orchestrates robot capabilities, each wrapped as a callable tool. It inherits the core components of coding agents, modified as the physical world requires. The world, however, withholds two abilities that software grants for free: reading the state of the world, and judging the outcome of an action. To bridge these gaps, Thea introduces Scene Graph as Context, a persistent, symbolic representation of the world, and Evaluation as Exit Codes, which detects when an action should terminate, judges whether it succeeded, and on failure diagnoses the cause. Together they close the loop between the agent and the physical world. Rich behaviors then emerge from the composition of tools, and the closed loop carries long-horizon tasks to completion in real environments.

</details>


### [126] [Conformity Mitigations in Large Language Models Lie on a Single Resistance-Receptivity Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11247)
*Zafar Hussain,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在23个开源大模型、19个条件、3个数据集（MMLU、GPQA、SimpleQA）上，通过超过100万次评分发现：一致性错误同行压力使模型放弃22.8%–71.0%的正确答案；现有的6种缓解方法（包括4种已有方法和2种新方法）都落在单一的"抵抗-接受"权衡前沿上（R²=0.80–0.90），即每获得一点抵抗错误同行的能力，就要牺牲一点接受正确同行的能力。唯一例外是"先推理再回答"——仅在答案可推导的MMLU科目上，同时提升两个指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中，模型会参考同行意见回答问题，但错误多数派可能推翻本来正确的答案。现有研究只关注"Resistance"（抗住错误同行的能力），却忽视了"Receptivity"（接受正确同行的能力）这一代价。作者旨在揭示一致性缓解措施的真实权衡，而非表面上的修复效果。

Method: 实验设计控制同行陈述不变，仅改变指令条件：(1) 23个开源权重模型（14B–70B，涵盖dense、MoE、hybrid架构）；(2) 19个条件包括无同行基线、5个逐步升级的同行压力、1个接受性探测，以及6种缓解方法在错误/正确两种同行极性下的测试；(3) 3个数据集：MMLU（多选题）、GPQA（科学难题）、SimpleQA（事实回忆）；(4) vLLM部署，温度0；(5) 固定脚本化的同行回答，由md5哈希决定错误选项；(6) SimpleQA由独立评判模型打分；(7) 用匹配问题的子样本比较，采用10,000次聚类自助法估计95%置信区间。

Result: (1) 同行压力效应：一致错误群体分别逆转MMLU的22.8%、GPQA的54.8%、SimpleQA的71.0%正确答案，84–89%的被改答案与同行回答一致；(2) 一致性与能力负相关而与模型规模无关（参数范围2.4B–70B）；(3) 6种方法均落在同一"抵抗-接受"前沿，R²在0.80–0.90之间，Reflection在MMLU上+7.9抗性/-15.3接受性；(4) 自创的Anchored Reconsideration也未能突破该前沿；(5) 唯一的例外是推理先行的方法：在MMLU的20个可推导科目上同时提升抗性+7.2和接受性+9.6（置信区间排除零），但在GPQA和SimpleQA上仍在线上。

Conclusion: 一致性缓解措施本质上是在单个权衡前沿上选择位置，而非真正修复问题。任务特性决定权衡——模型无法独立验证答案时，缓解措施就退化为零和权衡；只有推理（提供独立于同行的证据）才能在模型能完成推导的题目上同时提升两轴。这意味着设计多智能体系统时，应在能够独立验证的任务上路由推理，否则需明确报告在两个轴上的取舍。检索或工具调用可能成为更强的同类解决方案，但尚未验证。

Abstract: Recent advances in language models have enabled collaborative settings in which multiple models leverage one another's capabilities, iteratively improving, transforming, and extending each other's outputs. Each agent sees what the others assert before it answers, so peer opinion competes with the model's own parametric knowledge, and a wrong majority can overturn an answer the model would otherwise get right. We measure that displacement in 23 open-weight models, 19 conditions, and three datasets, yielding more than a million graded responses. A unanimous wrong majority reverses 22.8% of a model's correct MMLU answers, 54.8% on GPQA and 71.0% on SimpleQA, and 84-89% of the reversed answers match the peers' answers. Existing mitigations aim to increase Resistance, the rate at which a model keeps its correct answer under this pressure, which is only half of what a collaborating agent needs. We pair it with Receptivity, the rate at which a model adopts a correct peer answer after initially answering incorrectly. We score six methods on both axes, four drawn from prior work and two of our own. Each gains Resistance only by losing Receptivity, and their means fall on a single Resistance-Receptivity frontier with $R^2$ between 0.80 and 0.90. Reflection, the strongest published method, gains 7.9 points of MMLU Resistance and gives up 15.3 of Receptivity. Reasoning is the one exception. On GPQA and SimpleQA it trades like the rest, but on the MMLU subjects whose answers a model can derive for itself it raises Resistance by 7.2 points and Receptivity by 9.6 at once, the only intervention we find that improves both.

</details>


### [127] [EvoGraph-Mem: Failure-Aware Editable Graph Memory for Long-Term Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11248)
*Yuxi Qian,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoGraph-Mem提出了一种面向长期语言智能体的失败感知可编辑图记忆框架，通过为每个insight节点追踪正/负证据与激活状态，并引入图控制器在任务执行后执行保留、归档、修订与新增四种操作，解决现有append-only记忆系统中记忆污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强智能体主要关注存储与检索，但忽略了记忆质量的时序退化问题：先前提炼的insight可能在新的任务语境下变得过时、过度泛化甚至有害，反复检索这些记忆会造成"记忆污染"，进而损害长期推理性能。

Method: 1) 在G-Memory的insight图基础上扩展节点结构：每个insight节点包含内容κ、正证据集Ω⁺、负证据集Ω⁻以及激活状态z∈{0,1}；2) 提出效用感知检索机制，通过r=s−λc对候选insight同时考虑正证据与冲突负证据进行排序；3) 设计由prompt约束的LLM驱动的图控制器，在任务完成后对每个被检索的insight执行KEEP/ARCHIVE/REVISE三类编辑操作，并独立决定是否ADD新insight；4) 对修订操作保留旧节点为归档状态并创建新激活节点，使修正轨迹可追溯。

Result: 在PDDL、HotpotQA、FEVER三个基准上，使用GPT-4o-mini和Qwen2.5-7B两种骨干模型均取得一致提升：相对最强基线G-Memory，GPT-4o-mini平均提升9.81%，Qwen2.5-7B平均提升4.30%；消融实验表明add+keep+archive+revise完整控制器优于仅追加的add+keep变体，证明归档与修订操作的必要性；但token消耗相对无记忆基线增加约73%。

Conclusion: 该工作将长期智能体记忆从被动累积转向纠错性维护，验证了显式建模insight有效性并支持图层级编辑对长程任务的关键作用；其局限在于控制器依赖后置反馈、证据信号较粗、实验场景有限，且编辑图带来额外的计算与存储开销。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for language agents operating across extended interactions and evolving tasks. Existing memory-augmented agents mainly focus on storing and retrieving past experience, but the quality of stored memories may degrade over time. In particular, previously distilled insights can become outdated, over-generalized, or harmful under new task contexts, causing memory pollution when repeatedly reused. To address this issue, we study insight-level memory maintenance for long-term language agents and propose a failure-aware memory maintenance framework based on an editable insight graph. Each insight node tracks positive evidence, negative evidence, and an activation state, enabling the agent to distinguish reusable insights from conflicting or invalid ones. We further introduce a utility-aware retrieval mechanism and a graph controller that updates the memory graph after task execution by keeping reliable insights, archiving invalid ones, revising outdated ones, and adding newly discovered reusable insights. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms representative memory-based agent baselines across different backbone models. Ablation studies further demonstrate that append-only memory is insufficient for long-horizon tasks, while evidence-aware retrieval and graph-level editing improve memory reliability and downstream task performance.

</details>


### [128] [AgonAlpha: Autonomous Alpha Discovery via Prompt Economy and Scalable Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11250)
*Weicheng Ye,Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Shunyao Yu,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgonAlpha 将自动因子挖掘从“直接搜索公式”改造成“搜索可冻结、可复核的研究工件”：每个候选同时保存假设、可执行表达式、平台证据、经济解释和对抗审查结论。系统由提议者、独立上下文的审查者，以及可感知在途任务的 PW-MCTS 调度器组成；每次管线通过 16→8→4→2→1 的淘汰赛细化候选。在 WorldQuant BRAIN 的统一设置下，5 名使用者提交 60 个因子，其中 17 个获最高级 SPECTACULAR，最佳 Fitness 为 9.50、Sharpe 为 3.48。其主要价值是提供完整的提示词—搜索决策—平台记录—审查—表达式证据链；但该结果仍受单一市场、五年同窗搜索、样本内选择偏差、缺少消融与第三方复现等限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对 LLM 自动因子研究的三类系统性缺陷：其一，许多系统以公式或代码编辑为最小搜索单位，丢失假设、被拒方向、经济解释和审查状态，难以复盘；其二，LLM 自评不适合可靠排序已有研究指出的问题，而同一流程内自证又可能产生自确认偏差；其三，平台仿真昂贵且异步，固定轮次或仅按完成结果分配预算会浪费额度并忽略在途任务。作者因此把研究过程建模为需要证据治理和自适应资源分配的可审计发现循环。

Method: 搜索工件定义为 A=(h,f,E,r,v)，分别保存假设、FASTEXPR 表达式、平台仿真记录、经济理由和审查结论。提议者在祖先报告、工作目录、运行时资料和平台接口上生成 16 个候选，执行 16→8→4→2→1 的逐轮淘汰，并以绝对平台 Score 排序；近重复项在 0.85 自相关阈值下剔除，负收益优胜者通过符号翻转得到精确结果。独立路由且使用新上下文的审查者可重跑平台评估，只对经验证的不匹配、泄漏或伪造记录将奖励清零，其他经济解释、符号、常数、年度稳定性和冗余问题仅记录为警告。调度器以 PW-MCTS 在研究谱系间分配预算，将完成访问 v 与在途访问 π 分开，以公式 done_children(n)<k(v(n)+π(n))^α 限制扩展，并使用统计百分位奖励、UCB、背压和根回退避免忙碌死锁；固定参数为 C=10、k=1、α=0.5。单条管线通常调用 31 次仿真，而让 16 个初始候选全部经历五轮需 80 次，减少 61%，即候选版本数量约 2.6 倍。

Result: 在统一的 WorldQuant BRAIN 设置——美国 TOP3000 股票、延迟 1、2019–2023 评价窗口——5 名使用者和 6 个模型后端共提交 60 个因子，17 个获 SPECTACULAR（28.3%）；最佳 Fitness 9.50、Sharpe 3.48。5 个重点案例均为 SPECTACULAR，Fitness 范围 2.55–9.50、Sharpe 1.76–3.48、换手率 5.08%–9.58%、自相关均低于 0.70。审查者审计 24 份冻结报告，2 次确认证据缺陷并将调度奖励降为零；另有 11 项保留风险警告。系统还在同一棵搜索树下通过 10 个并发提议者—审查者管线的部署。论文进一步公开了角色提示词、调度器状态、候选报告、仿真输入与响应、相关性、提交检查、审查文本和最终可执行表达式。五类代表信号分别利用期权需求错位、成交量稳定性、持续做空兴趣、多期限看跌保护需求和远期曲线状态条件；最佳节点 KPE0LnN1 的年度 Fitness 为 8.43、2.59、18.28、17.19、4.15，显示虽非单年结果，但 2021–2022 仍占主导，最大与最小年度值相差 7.1 倍。

Conclusion: AgonAlpha 展示了一个可审计的最小自动研究架构：仅用提议者与对抗审查者两个角色、101 行角色提示词，以及工件级搜索和两级预算分配，就能把 LLM 公式生成转化为带有证据门禁和研究谱系的研究过程。相较只给赢家公式的系统，它公开了失败节点、审查否决和完整执行轨迹，这是其主要贡献；相较自建回测，使用作者无法修改的 BRAIN 数据、规则和评分也增强了端到端外部验证。不过，论文提供的是架构与生产平台可运行性证据，而非严格的算法优越性证据。缺少与随机搜索、固定预算、纯 LLM 或人工流程的受控消融，不能单独证明 PW-MCTS、对抗审查和提示词设计分别带来多少增益；五名使用者均为共同作者、单一股票池与 2019–2023 同一窗口限制了外部效度，平台 OS 跟踪仅说明进入跟踪阶段而未报告后续表现。此外，复杂幂次、窗口和大量候选调参会造成数据窥探与过拟合，年度报告和作者自己的警告也显示明显状态依赖。因此，该工作强有力地支持“完整、可审查的自动因子发现循环是可实现的”，但尚不能证明这些信号在未见时期、市场或真实执行条件下具有稳定增量收益。

Abstract: Language models can propose many plausible trading factors, but an autonomous research system must also allocate its evaluation budget, verify its own evidence, and preserve how each candidate was produced. We present AgonAlpha, an architecture that searches over frozen research artifacts---hypotheses, executable expressions, platform evidence, rationales, and review status---rather than formulas alone. To our knowledge, AgonAlpha is the first alpha-mining system to combine verified artifact search, a fresh-context adversarial reviewer with re-execution and veto authority, and pending-aware parallel budget allocation, together with a complete public evidence trail. Independent deployments on WorldQuant BRAIN produced SPECTACULAR-grade alphas across five users and six model backends, with Fitness reaching 9.50 and Sharpe reaching 3.48, while retaining prompt-to-expression provenance for every submission.

</details>


### [129] [Local verification cannot detect non-transportability: a cohomological theory of context preservation in agentic reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11252)
*Suyash Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文证明当前智能体（agentic AI）系统所采用的"局部验证"策略存在结构性盲区：通过将上下文空间建模为覆盖的神经（nerve），证据建模为1-上链（1-cochain），作者证明智能体的推理等价于路径积分，结论的路径无关性当且仅当证据上链是恰当的（exact），而路径间分歧恰好是第一Čech上同调类的全纯不变量（holonomy）。Hodge分解将证据冲突分为梯度（校准）、旋度（局部不一致）和调和（结构不可迁移性）三部分；中心结论是任何单纯形支撑的一致性检验都无法检测调和部分。基于此提出Kṣetra方法，通过上边缘投影估计、以调和能量为门控进行弃权，并在药物和信贷两个领域进行2,400次模拟验证，以及外汇套利检测的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI系统（如Medea）通过局部验证——检查实体可表示性、参数兼容性、输出与计划一致性——来保障跨生物、临床和金融上下文的结论可靠性。然而这类检查是"局部的"（仅支撑于上下文空间的单纯形上），其检测能力边界未被理论刻画。网络Meta分析中已知的"环路不一致性"现象提示了潜在的结构性缺陷，但缺乏一般性的形式化框架。作者旨在回答：局部验证能检测什么、不能检测什么，以及如何超越这一限制。

Method: 1. **理论建模**：将上下文空间建模为站点（site），覆盖𝒰的神经N(𝒰)为单纯复形；证据为1-上链ω（每个重叠上的对比估计）；主张赋值θ∈C⁰。相干性要求ω=δ⁰θ。
2. **路径积分与全纯不变量**（定理5）：智能体沿路径γ传输的估计θ̂ᵗᵞ=θₐ+⟨ω,γ⟩；路径无关性⟺ω∈im δ⁰；不同路径分歧=⟨ω,γ-γ'⟩=[ω]∈H¹的全纯不变量。
3. **Hodge分解与三分类**（定理7）：C¹=im δ⁰ ⊕ ℋ¹ ⊕ im(δ¹)ᵀ正交分解——梯度g（校准偏移）、旋度c（三重重叠局部不一致）、调和h（结构不可迁移性）。
4. **局部验证不完备性**（推论9）：单纯形支撑的一致性检验无法区分ω与ω+h（h∈ℋ¹），检测H¹需要环路基上的统计量。
5. **Kṣetra过程**：构建站点→装配证据→Hodge分解→三分类分诊（重校准/重测量/细化覆盖）→上边缘投影估计→以‖h‖/√|E|为门控弃权。
6. **精确F检验**（§7）：自由度精确分解|E|=(|V|-k)+rank δ¹+β₁；F=（‖h‖²/β₁）/（‖c‖²/rank δ¹）∼F(β₁, rank δ¹)。
7. **精度白化**（§8.2）：当精度各向异性时，用D=diag(1/σₑ)加权恢复精确性。
8. **机制建模**（§8）：θ_{c,s}=μ_c+λ_s+mγ_{c,s}，调和能量在m=0时为机器精度1.52×10⁻¹⁵，非零时单调增长。
9. **实验**：药物RWE（CKD分期×医疗机构，4×4网格）与消费信贷（地区×宏观经济周期，圆环拓扑）；1,200次重复×2领域；5种智能体对比。

Result: 1. **理论结果**：定理5证明路径无关性⟺恰当性；定理7给出Hodge三分量的操作意义；推论9证明局部验证的结构不完备性；命题12证明细化覆盖是唯一补救。
2. **路径依赖性验证**（声明A）：调和与旋度能量均与路径分歧显著相关（ρ=0.350–0.423，p<10⁻⁴），梯度不相关。
3. **不可约误差预测**（声明B）：仅调和能量预测最优（上边缘投影）估计器的不可约误差（ρ=0.311/0.471，p<10⁻⁴）；旋度与梯度不显著。
4. **选择性性能**（表2）：Kṣetra在两个领域均优于最强基线（残差门控），ΔAURC=-0.029（药物）和-0.039（信贷），p<0.001；有害率@75%覆盖率分别降低0.032和0.043。单链智能体反而差于上下文盲的合并智能体（MAE 1.82 vs 0.83），证实路径链接放大方差。
5. **冲突分诊**：约24.6%（药物）/29.0%（信贷）的查询因H¹阻塞而需细化覆盖。
6. **漂移成本**（§5.5）：仅30%查询静默粗化即导致MAE 0.134与9.0%有害率。
7. **F检验校准**：在4,000次零假设复制中精确（KS D=0.018, p=0.135）；在η=0.30处功效0.62，η=0.60处0.88。
8. **外汇应用**（§7.2）：调和监测在1bp检测环路套利（AUROC 0.997），而总残差监测需4bp才达0.95；G10子复形H¹=0证实三角套利监控完备性。
9. **ECL失真**（§7.3）：IFRS 9/SR 11-7场景中，单链路由失真线性增长至79bp，投影估计保持5bp；发现17个独立环路。
10. **机制验证**：调和能量在m=0时为1.52×10⁻¹⁵（机器零），随m单调增长；预测ρ=0.367（p<10⁻⁴⁸），控制旋度和梯度后偏相关系数0.19（p<10⁻¹³）。
11. **精度白化**：4倍精度差异下未加权F检验膨胀至7.1%，白化后恢复至4.7%。
12. **证据缺口效应**：缺口隐藏阻塞而非制造阻塞；不完全神经的dim H¹为真实β₁的下界。

Conclusion: 该工作将智能体AI的上下文保持问题严格形式化为1-上链相干性问题，证明了局部验证（单纯形支撑的一致性检验）存在不可避免的盲区——调和分量H¹代表结构不可迁移性，无法被任何局部检查检测，但产生真实的路径间分歧。基于此提出的Kṣetra方法通过Hodge分解将冲突分为三类（重校准、重测量、细化覆盖），并以调和能量单独门控弃权，在药物RWE和消费信贷两个结构不同的领域中均优于最强基线。精确F检验提供了存在全局主张的统计判据，外汇案例表明调和监测可在1bp检测传统残差监测需4bp才能发现的环路套利。该框架将弃权从置信启发式转变为可计算、可审计、可复现的拓扑判定，并为治理框架（如SR 11-7）提供具有环路见证的实质性工件。主要局限包括：仅模拟验证无真实数据、未处理重叠误差的相关性、上下文因子分解的获取无理论指导、仅适用实值主张；作者明确指出下一步应在Medea的酵母E-MAP真实数据和已发表网络Meta分析上验证。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems routinely transport conclusions across biological, clinical and financial contexts, and the emerging safeguard is local verification: checking at each step that the entity is representable in the chosen tool, that parameters are compatible, and that outputs cohere with the plan. We prove this class of safeguard is structurally incomplete. Modelling a covering of context space by its nerve and evidence by a real-valued 1-cochain, an agent chaining evidence performs path integration: its conclusion is path-independent if and only if the cochain is exact, and disagreement between valid reasoning paths is exactly the holonomy of a first Cech cohomology class. Hodge decomposition partitions evidence conflict into a gradient part (calibration), a curl part (local inconsistency, visible at triple overlaps) and a harmonic part. Our central result is that no family of simplex-supported consistency checks can distinguish omega from omega+h for harmonic h, which nonetheless generates non-zero disagreement between valid paths; detection requires a statistic on a cycle basis. The resulting procedure, Ksetra, estimates by coboundary projection and gates abstention on the harmonic component, which we give a mechanism: it arises from effect modification combined with overlap-specific population composition, and vanishes to machine precision when effect modification is absent. The degrees of freedom of an evidence network partition into calibration, coherence and transport, yielding an exact F-test for the existence of a global claim; we quantify its distortion under unequal precision and supply the precision-whitened form that restores exactness. Foreign exchange, where the arbitrage-free null makes the cochain exactly a coboundary, serves as a calibration bench: the test is correctly sized, fires on loop arbitrage, and ignores triangular arbitrage.

</details>


### [130] [Symbolic Machine Learning for Vapor-Liquid Equilibrium Prediction in Cx-N2 Binary Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11255)
*Bongseok Kim,Suman Chakraborty,Gary Huang,Mehek Mathur,Guang Lin,Li Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层次符号回归框架，用于改进Peng-Robinson状态方程(PR-EOS)对碳氢化合物-氮气二元体系汽液平衡(VLE)的预测精度。该方法通过两级策略——先为各碳氢化合物系统独立发现符号修正表达式，再将其系数建模为碳数的函数——构建了兼顾可解释性与跨系统泛化能力的统一符号修正模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PR-EOS在高压和近临界条件下对碳氢化合物-氮气混合物的VLE预测存在显著偏差，尤其在宽组成范围和不同碳链长度下表现不佳。深度学习模型虽能提升精度，但缺乏可解释性和显式解析表达式，难以融入传统热力学建模流程。因此需要一种既能保持PR-EOS物理基础，又能提供可解释闭式表达式的修正方法。

Method: 采用两级符号机器学习策略：(1) 系统特定符号回归——使用PySR进化搜索算法（包含变异、交叉、迁移三种遗传算子），针对六个C₅–C₁₂正烷烃+N₂系统，分别独立发现压力残差ΔP和气相组成残差Δy的符号修正表达式；(2) 多层次符号回归——从系统特定表达式中提取反复出现的共享符号基函数（线性-指数、耦合多项式、指数非线性、逆温度等形式），并将基函数系数参数化为碳数n_C的函数（通过多项式插值），形成统一的修正模型。最终将ΔP和Δy加到PR-EOS原始预测上获得修正结果。

Result: 在660个实验数据点（六个系统：C₅H₁₂、C₆H₁₄、C₇H₁₆、C₉H₂₀、C₁₀H₂₂、C₁₂H₂₆与N₂的二元体系，覆盖5–60 MPa、313.6–593.5 K范围）上，所提两级符号回归模型显著优于原始PR-EOS和传统单层符号回归：压力预测测试集MSE从PR-EOS的9.40降至1.23×10⁻¹，R²从0.9528提升至0.9994；气相组成预测测试集MSE从6.41×10⁻⁴降至3.77×10⁻⁵，R²从0.9762提升至0.9986。P-x相图、临界点附近以及全系统综合parity图均显示修正模型与实验数据高度吻合，且对训练集和测试集均保持良好一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种可解释的符号修正框架，通过共享符号基函数与碳数依赖系数函数的两级分解，在保留PR-EOS物理结构和闭式表达式的同时，显著提升了跨多个碳氢化合物-氮气系统的VLE预测精度。未来工作将探索方程层面的符号修正，即直接修改状态方程的解析形式以保持物理一致性。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of vapor--liquid equilibrium (VLE) for hydrocarbon-nitrogen mixtures remains challenging for cubic equations of state, particularly across broad ranges of composition and hydrocarbon chain length. While deep learning models can provide accurate predictions, they often lack interpretability and explicit analytical expressions. In this work, we propose a symbolic machine learning approach to discover interpretable symbolic corrections to Peng-Robinson equation-of-state (PR-EOS) predictions from experimental data. The proposed approach adopts a two-level strategy: symbolic expressions are first identified for individual hydrocarbon systems, after which their coefficients are represented as functions of carbon number to enable accurate prediction across different hydrocarbon systems. The results demonstrate significantly improved prediction accuracy over the original PR-EOS across all hydrocarbon-nitrogen systems. Overall, the proposed approach provides an interpretable symbolic correction framework for improving PR-EOS predictions of hydrocarbon-nitrogen VLE.

</details>


### [131] [Adaptive Hybrid Particle Swarm Optimization with Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11258)
*Aryan Gurudeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出自适应混合粒子群优化算法（AHPSO），通过sigmoid函数基于种群多样性自动调节梯度下降的影响权重，在探索阶段几乎关闭梯度影响，在开发阶段接近最大化，无需手动阶段切换。研究表明，在迭代匹配比较中AHPSO-Adadelta在9种方法中排名第一（含CMA-ES），但在预算归一化比较中纯PSO胜率52.5% vs AHPSO的20%，说明梯度注入的价值是有条件的，仅在平滑局部盆地问题上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSO擅长全局探索但收敛慢，梯度下降（GD）收敛快但易陷入局部最优。已有混合方法存在两个核心问题：（1）何时注入梯度（即"时机"问题）；（2）手工设定切换阈值或固定调度缺乏自适应性。本文的核心动机是：当且仅当种群已定位到具有平滑局部结构的盆地时，梯度信息才有价值；过早或在崎岖景观上应用梯度只会增加成本而无收益。需要一个原则性机制自动判断注入时机。

Method: 核心方法AHPSO包含四个关键设计：
1. **多样性度量**：计算粒子群在d维空间中各维度位置标准方差的平均值D(t)，作为种群收敛状态的实时信号。
2. **自适应梯度权重**：通过sigmoid函数α(t) = α_min + (1-α_min)/(1+exp(k(D(t)/D(0)-τ)))将多样性比值映射到[α_min, 1]区间，其中τ=0.3（多样性降至初始30%时切换）、k=5（过渡陡度）、α_min=0.1（最小梯度影响）。
3. **数值梯度估计**：使用中心差分∂f/∂x_j ≈ [f(x+εe_j)-f(x-εe_j)]/(2ε)估计黑盒函数的梯度，每粒子每迭代额外消耗2d次函数评估。
4. **可选优化器集成**：测试了6种一阶优化器（SGD、Adagrad、RMSprop、Adam、Nadam、Adadelta）作为梯度步进组件，在每代PSO更新后对每个粒子施加α(t)·GD_step的修正。

Result: 在29个基准函数、42种配置、14,700次独立运行的实验中发现：
1. **迭代匹配比较**：Friedman检验显示AHPSO-Adadelta在9种方法（含CMA-ES、CLPSO）中排名第一（χ²(8)=26.19, p=9.75×10⁻⁴），所有自适应学习率混合方法均优于标准PSO。
2. **预算归一化比较**：当给予PSO等量函数评估预算时，PSO在40种配置中胜出52.5%（21项），AHPSO仅胜出20%（8项），差异显著（p=7.0×10⁻⁵）；AHPSO仅在平滑盆地问题（F8、F24-F27）上保持优势。
3. **逐组分析**：单峰函数梯度可靠，混合方法获益最大；多模态函数混合效果中性；旋转复合函数梯度信息因轴对齐差而失效；SGD因固定学习率在惩罚函数上发散而排名垫底。
4. **消融实验**：自适应sigmoid在单峰（F1）和惩罚函数（F12）上优于所有固定α值，在多模态函数上略逊于特定固定值，优势在于跨问题类型的鲁棒性而非单函数最优。
5. **工程应用**：在焊接梁设计和压力容器设计中，CMA-ES占优（4维问题），AHPSO优于纯PSO但优势有限。AHPSO实际定位为中等维度（10≤d≤30）且目标函数廉价场景。
6. **参数敏感性**：τ∈[0.2, 0.4]范围内性能稳定，sigmoid机制对阈值选择不敏感。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是原则性地刻画了梯度注入在群体智能搜索中提供价值的条件，而非宣称普遍优越性。AHPSO通过多样性驱动的sigmoid门控机制，自动且连续地调节梯度影响，无需手动阶段切换，并具备自纠正特性（多样性回升时梯度影响自动降低）。然而，方法存在四个明确局限：（1）中心差分带来2d倍函数评估开销（d=30时达61×），对昂贵仿真不实用；（2）sigmoid中点τ=0.3基于本基准调优；（3）依赖函数连续性；（4）双学习率方案依赖问题类别先验。最佳实践推荐：若能负担2d额外评估，将PSO与Adadelta配对为目标函数廉价且具有平滑局部结构的中等维度问题。

Abstract: Gradient injection helps Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) only when the swarm has identified a basin with smooth local structure, not universally. We propose Adaptive Hybrid PSO (AHPSO), which uses a sigmoid function on swarm diversity to automatically modulate gradient influence: near-zero during exploration, near-maximum during exploitation, with no manual phase-switching. Under budget-normalized comparison (PSO given equivalent total function evaluations), PSO wins 52.5% of 40 configurations versus AHPSO's 20% (p = 7.0e-5, Friedman). AHPSO retains advantage specifically on problems with smooth local basins (F8, F24-F27) where directed descent outperforms undirected sampling even at equal cost. Under iteration-matched comparison across 29 functions (42 configurations, 14,700 runs), AHPSO-Adadelta ranks first of 9 methods including CMA-ES (p = 9.75e-4). The contribution is a principled characterization of when gradient injection provides value in swarm-based search, not a claim of universal superiority.

</details>


### [132] [Glance, Scrutinize, and Think: Advancing Video Anomaly Detection from Training-Free to Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11260)
*Shibo Gao,Peipei Yang,Xu-Yao Zhang,Linlin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对视频异常检测（VAD）中"何时-何物"能力割裂的问题，提出统一的全局到局部推理范式，并从两个互补视角展开研究：训练自由的GtS框架与工具增强的智能体VAD方法。同时构建了大规模数据集VAGU-T及联合评估指标JeAUG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAD方法存在显著的"when-what"能力割裂：传统DNN方法能定位异常时间区间（when）但缺乏语义理解；LLM方法能解释异常内容（what）但忽视精确时间定位。此外，VLM逐段处理方法计算开销过大，难以满足实时需求。作者认为根本原因在于缺乏统一的推理范式，并借鉴人类监控视频的认知过程——先全局扫视形成时间假设，再局部细查可疑片段，最后迭代思考修正错误——提出全局到局部的统一框架。

Method: 方法一（GtS，训练自由）：双阶段粗到细框架。"Glance"阶段利用静态（主体/场景）和动态（动作/事件）文本引导，通过CLIP和Video-CLIP双编码器计算相似度曲线，经Savitzky-Golay滤波平滑后采用三阶段分割策略定位高概率异常区间；"Scrutinize"阶段在高概率段进行非均匀采样并通过VQA/VTG模型完成细粒度异常理解与时序定位。

方法二（工具增强智能体VAD）：将全局到局部推理循环内化到单个多模态大语言模型中。采用两阶段训练策略：(1) 冷启动SFT，基于VAGU-T的思维链工具调用轨迹数据建立基础工具调用能力；(2) 基于GRPO的智能体强化学习，设计包含准确度奖励（LLM-as-a-Judge三档评分）、格式奖励（严格的think/tool_call/answer结构）和时序定位奖励（IoU）的联合奖励。

数据集VAGU-T：7,567个真实异常视频，21类异常，四步人工标注流程，新增工具调用推理轨迹构建。

评估指标JeAUG：联合考虑异常理解分数（LLM多维评分）与异常定位分数（基于人类偏好对齐的对数分段函数），并引入视频长度补偿因子。

Result: 在VAGU-T基准上：(1) GtS显著超越训练自由基线，以Qwen2.5-VL-7B为骨干时JeAUG从2.28提升至4.04，FPS保持61；(2) 智能体RL模型取得最优性能，A.U.达7.35，JeAUG达5.91，FPS达148，远超实时阈值30；(3) SFT模型已超越逐段处理方法SUVAD（A.U. 6.62 vs 5.73），速度快约三个数量级；(4) 消融实验证实GtS各组件、RL奖励各分量均贡献正向增益，R_time的引入带来0.42的A.U.提升；(5) 类别分析显示GtS对显著多主体事件（如抢劫、斗殴）提升最大，而智能体RL对细微短时异常（如交通违规、商店行窃）提升最显著，验证了两方法互补性。

Conclusion: 论文系统性地从统一全局到局部视角研究了视频异常定位与理解的联合问题，提出的VAGU-T数据集首次集成了异常类别、语义解释、时序定位、QA及思维链工具调用轨迹；GtS框架和工具增强智能体方法分别从训练自由和训练学习两个互补角度实现了该范式，在精度-效率权衡上均优于现有方法；JeAUG指标克服了传统单维度评估的局限。作者认为该工作将推动大语言模型时代VAD研究的进一步发展。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) aims to identify anomalous events and localize their temporal intervals. Existing approaches exhibit a "when-what" dissociation: traditional DNN-based methods localize when anomalies occur but lack semantic understanding, whereas LLM-based methods explain what happens but neglect precise temporal grounding. We attribute this to the absence of a unified reasoning paradigm. Inspired by how humans inspect surveillance videos - glancing globally to form temporal hypotheses, scrutinizing suspicious segments, and thinking iteratively to correct errors - we study this global-to-local paradigm from two perspectives. We first propose Glance then Scrutinize (GtS), a training-free framework using static and dynamic textual guidance for coarse-to-fine anomaly grounding and understanding, balancing accuracy and speed. To break the ceiling imposed by frozen external modules, we further propose a tool-augmented agentic VAD method, where a multimodal large language model learns to invoke a video cropping tool, inspect densely resampled frames, and self-correct mislocalized hypotheses, via cold-start supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with a joint answer-grounding reward. For training and evaluation, we extend our prior VAGU benchmark into VAGU-T (Video Anomaly Grounding, Understanding, and Thinking), comprising 7,567 real-world videos over 21 anomaly categories with human-validated grounding, explanations, QA pairs, and chain-of-thought tool-calling traces. We further introduce JeAUG, a metric jointly evaluating semantic interpretability and temporal precision. Experiments show that GtS substantially surpasses training-free baselines, while the agentic model delivers both higher accuracy and faster inference.

</details>


### [133] [Deployment Decision Reliability: A Generalizability-Theory Framework for Sizing Long-Horizon Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11323)
*Vasundra Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"部署决策可靠性"（DDR）框架，运用四因子一般化理论（G-theory）方差的方差分解，在三个企业级智能体基准（TheAgentCompany、τ²-bench、AppWorld）上证明智能体主效应仅占不到3%的总方差，而智能体×任务交互占7–23%，揭示排行榜排名的是"专长化"而非"通用能力"。同时发现四个警示性现象：困难任务上可靠性崩溃、训练单元格可靠性与留出可靠性负相关（r=−0.90）、总体诊断可迁移但家族级排名反转、MAST失效模式的逐trace配置文件不可泛化而逐单元格可泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业采购智能体时将排行榜分数视为能力排序依据，但这些分数将测量变异的多重来源压缩成单一数字，无法区分分数差距来源于智能体本身、任务抽样、轨迹截断步骤、失效类别或其交互。已有心理测量学传统（G-theory）长期处理此类测量设计问题，但尚未应用于多步骤智能体这一测量对象——轨迹引入了步骤位置与失效类别作为新的因子，且部署决策面临成本与难度约束。

Method: 采用四因子（智能体a、任务t、步骤s、错误类别e）完全交叉G-study设计，将每次观察建模为二值成功指标Y_{atse}，使用三种估计器交叉验证：Henderson方法I（闭式ANOVA）、REML（lme4）、贝叶斯二项GLMM（bambi/numpyro在NVIDIA L4 GPU上）。计算一般化系数Eρ²（相对排序）与依赖性系数Φ（绝对阈值）；提出能力-缺口比σ²_a/(σ²_a+σ²_{a:t})；引入可靠性/美元（RPD）成本感知指标；按任务难度四分位做条件化可靠性分析；执行50次随机70/30训练/测试分裂检验留出泛化性；使用MAST/MAD数据集做失效模式方差分解与跨数据集对比。使用数据集：TheAgentCompany（17智能体×175任务，7,783行）、τ²-bench（3智能体×50任务×5检查类型）、AppWorld（18配置×79,650单元测试行）、MAD/MAST（1,242 LLM判定轨迹×14失效模式）。

Result: 核心实证发现：（1）能力上限——σ²_a在每个数据集/检查类型中不到3%，σ²_{a:t}占比7.5–12.5%，能力-缺口比普遍低于0.19（TheAgentCompany上<0.001）；（2）困难任务崩溃——τ² action_checks的Eρ²从0.752降至Q4的0.000；（3）留出反转——τ²上训练单元格Eρ²与留出Eρ²的皮尔逊相关r=−0.90；（4）总体vs家族不对称——能力-缺口比在TheAgentCompany（0.38）、AppWorld test_normal（0.40）、test_challenge（0.35）间稳定，但共享模型家族的Spearman ρ=−0.50；（5）方差≠频率——τ² σ²_{a:t}与MAST激活率逐类Spearman ρ=−0.20。MAST上trace×mode交互占71.8%方差，trace级MAE=0.261，单元格级MAE=0.056（r=0.83）。成本感知RPD在τ²上反转o4-mini与GPT-4.1排名。三估计器一致到三位小数，PSIS-LOO敏感性分析证实主要结论对建模选择稳健。

Conclusion: 提出DDR作为一页式报告规范，将方差分量表转化为企业采购方可辩护的五项决策：采样多少任务、瞄准哪个难度桶、受何成本带约束、向基准供应商要求何留出协议、如何解读失效模式分类法。核心实践建议：（1）勿将排行榜排名本身视为采购信号；（2）向供应商要求难度条件化可靠性数字（Eρ²_{Q4}）；（3）始终将训练单元格可靠性预测与留出交叉验证估计配对报告。多模型编排架构在测量理论上得到正当性证明——若无智能体统一胜出，采购正确决策是按任务类路由而非单一选型。三项局限：跨数据集秩相关分析功效不足（四类别、三家族）；步骤交换性假设不适用于自由形式轨迹分段；σ²_a≈0的发现限于当前前沿智能体种群，不排除未来架构重新打开能力差距。

Abstract: Enterprise practitioners read agent leaderboards as if they ranked agent capability. We show, across three open agent-trace benchmarks (TheAgentCompany, $\tau^2$-bench, and AppWorld), that the agent main effect accounts for less than 3% of total variance in every dataset and check type, while the agent-by-task interaction accounts for 7-23%. Leaderboards rank specialization, not capability. We arrive at this through a four-facet Generalizability Theory variance decomposition, fit with three estimators (Henderson Method-I, REML via lme4, and a Bayesian binomial GLMM) that agree to three decimal places. Four further findings sharpen what the leaderboard is hiding. First, aggregate reliability collapses on the hardest task quartile: $E\rho^2$ on $\tau^2$ action_checks falls from 0.752 to 0.000. Second, training-cell reliability negatively correlates with held-out reliability ($r = -0.90$ on $\tau^2$), meaning the designs that look most reliable replicate worst. Third, population-level diagnostics transfer across enterprise benchmarks (capability-gap ratio stable at 0.35-0.40) but per-family agent rankings invert. Fourth, on the MAST failure taxonomy, trace-level mode profiles are idiosyncratic (MAE = 0.261) while cell-level profiles generalise (MAE = 0.056, $r = 0.83$). We package these into Deployment Decision Reliability (DDR), a one-page reporting discipline that turns the variance-component table into five decisions an enterprise buyer can defend. All code, data loaders, and fit artifacts are released under an open-source license.

</details>


### [134] [Apodex Discovery: Reality Benchmarks and Environments for Evaluating and Building Discoverative Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11341)
*Brian Wang,Bin Feng,Xiaoman Pan,Chenyang An,Felix Liu,Tangqi Fang,Gongbo Sun,Lingfeng Shen,Ning Wang,Handuo Zhang,Feng Chen,Fuchao Yang,Xiang Wang,Jiacheng Lin,Siting Li,Zixuan Liu,Chi Han,Zhenhailong Wang,Kunlun Zhu,Lawrence Zhao,Yueqi Guo,Kailong Wen,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Lidong Bing,David Tan,Bo An,Heng Ji,Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Apodex Discovery框架，用于构建和评估"发现型AI"(discoverative AI)，通过引入重型求解器(heavy-duty solver)、现实基准TRACES以及六维过程验证指标HDS6（对应TRACES：工具、修复、备选方案、一致性、证据、范围），将AI评估从静态问答拓展为基于可执行环境、可验证过程与可修复循环的真实世界发现任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估范式存在两个根本局限：(1) 静态问答基准（如MMLU、GPQA、FrontierMath）仅评估固定提示到正确答案的映射，无法衡量工具使用、状态维持与失败修复等长程推理能力；(2) 交互式基准（如SWE-bench、WebArena）虽然引入了可执行环境，但通常局限于狭义任务、最终成功信号单一，且无法评估求解过程的可靠性（如问题分解、证据基础、备选假设管理）。最具现实意义的开放性问题（如新药研发、新材料设计、可控核聚变）很少以可执行或可验证的形式呈现，而前沿模型在这些问题定义清晰、工具与成功标准明确后已能解决复杂任务。因此，构建将开放性现实问题转化为可执行、可验证、可修复的发现任务的基础设施，是AI向真正科学发现过渡的关键缺口。

Method: 论文提出三层核心方法论：(1) **系统性问题发现流程**：由10位STEM博士组成的团队对16个领域、561个行业进行调查，筛选出423个高价值问题，按"技术契合度、价值、商业可及性"三维独立评分（取三者最大值而非平均），最终从423个候选问题中精选20个进入初始TRACES基准，其中包含回溯型任务（隐藏已知结果）和前瞻型发现挑战（无权威答案）。(2) **可执行环境抽象**：建立environment-task-episode三层抽象，通过manifest声明问题目标、任务分解、隐藏验证器、数据/工具/资源预算；episode作为具体可运行实例，通过固定的episode接口与solver系统交互，确保结果差异可归因于solver组件而非环境。(3) **双重验证机制**：outcome verification（对最终提交按隐藏真值评分，含反作弊防泄漏硬门，归一化到[0,1]区间）与process verification并行；后者通过HDS6六个维度（Tools、Repair、Alternatives、Coherence、Evidence、Scope）的25个子能力对完整轨迹进行盲评（不知结果、不知求解器身份、剥离思维链），采用子评分表+承重步骤分析两种方式，由提议-反驳-裁决三阶段判定流程评分，并产出结构化修复笔记返回solver形成测量-诊断-改进闭环。

Result: 在AAV衣壳设计四个子任务（活性预测、趋向性预测、结构预测、生成式设计）上，apodex-1.1全部超越已发表最优模型，平均超出7%（活性0.904 vs 0.878、趋向性0.635 vs 0.622、结构0.649 vs 0.605、生成设计0.180 vs 0.116），且使用领域专用环境的claude-opus-4-8比通用Claude Code harness平均提升（0.741 vs 0.716）。在药物重定位任务上，加入生物医学环境后GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6-sol的归一化预测评分分别提升2.52和7.60分。在LLM相关任务上，跨6个基础模型与4个模型无关harness的消融显示无单一harness普优：ApodexHarness在Opus上最优（0.611），A-Evolve在GPT上最优（0.648），证明harness设计与模型存在显著交互作用。验证驱动的修复在434条HDS6<3的轨迹上平均带来+0.155的结果分数提升，9/10环境为正向改进，验证了从过程诊断到结果提升的闭环有效性。HDS6与结果分数的Spearman相关性在0.24-0.65之间（pooled ρ=+0.51），证明盲评的过程分数与隐藏真值具有显著一致性。

Conclusion: 论文通过TRACES基准（17个可执行环境、218个episode、覆盖生物医学、临床转化与前沿模型工程）和Apodex Discovery框架，将AI评估的单元从孤立答案转向完整可验证、可修复的发现episode：固定episode接口使性能差异可归因到具体的solver组件（基础模型、harness、agent loop、初始技能指导、验证器驱动修复）。该工作定义了发现型AI的"探索-行动-验证-修正"闭环范式，为AI从执行预设基准走向进行真实世界发现的研究提供了基础设施与方法论。局限在于当前仅实现了20个精选问题中的17个环境与218个episode，且过程验证与结果分数的对齐仍需在更广泛应用中验证；扩展方向包括将更多423个候选问题转化为可执行环境。

Abstract: Apollo did not reach the Moon merely because its engineers could solve difficult equations. It succeeded by turning a distant ambition into a mission architecture of explicit objectives, simulation, verification, and repeated correction. AI now faces a similar transition: frontier models can solve difficult tasks once the problem, tools, and success criteria are specified, yet consequential real-world challenges rarely arrive in an executable or verifiable form. We introduce Apodex Discovery, a framework for building and evaluating discoverative AI through the heavy-duty solver, a system comprising a foundation model, harness, tools, and control policies that pursues extended, stateful, verifiable investigations. It has three core components. First, a problem-scouting process surveyed 561 industries across 16 sectors, assembled 423 high-value real-world problems, and selected 20 for the initial release. Second, a common environment-task-episode abstraction provides data, tools, constraints, feedback, trajectory recording, and verification of intermediate artifacts and final submissions. Third, HDS6 evaluates Tools, Repair, Alternatives, Coherence, Evidence, and Scope independently of final-task success. In AAV capsid design, Apodex surpassed the published state of the art by 7% across viability, tropism, structure prediction, and generative design. In drug repurposing and reformulation, a task-specific biomedical environment improved the mean normalized prediction score of GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6-sol by 2.5 and 7.6 points over the same closed-book backbone. Controlled ablations show that the fixed TRACES episode interface enables attribution of performance differences to specific solver components. Apodex Discovery moves AI evaluation beyond predefined benchmarks toward verifiable investigations aimed at genuine discovery.

</details>


### [135] [Can Frontier LLMs Match Natively Multimodal Embeddings? A Comparison on Hard-Negative Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11343)
*Archan Dutta,Vyanktesh Kanungo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在 Flickr30k 硬负样本集上首次系统比较了原生多模态嵌入模型（Gemini Embedding 2、Amazon Nova 2）与前沿大语言模型（GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4.6）作为零样本视觉排序器的检索性能，发现三者在精度上无统计显著差异，但嵌入模型在预计算后具有四个数量级的速度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 Gemini Embedding 2 等原生多模态嵌入模型和具备视觉理解能力的前沿 LLM 的相继出现，多模态检索领域出现了两种新范式：原生多模态嵌入（将多模态映射到统一嵌入空间）与 LLM 直接视觉排序（在单次提示中联合推理候选图像）。然而，这两种范式在硬负样本条件下的检索精度与排序延迟尚未被直接比较，存在关键研究空白。

Method: 研究采用 Flickr30k 数据集的 5,000 张图像池，通过分层抽样为每张图像分配一个代表性标题，并使用 sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2 在标题嵌入空间中为每条查询构建 25 张候选图像的硬负样本集（1 张正确图像 + 24 张最相似负样本）。从中随机抽取 1,000 条查询，分别送入四个系统：(1) Gemini Embedding 2 与 Amazon Nova 2 通过计算查询-候选对的余弦相似度独立排序；(2) GPT-4.1 与 Claude Sonnet 4.6 在单个提示中同时接收 25 张图像进行联合视觉推理排序（候选顺序随机化以控制位置偏差）。评估指标包括 Recall@1、Recall@3 和 MRR，配以 95% Bootstrap 置信区间、McNemar 检验和 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验（Bonferroni 校正，α=0.05）。

Result: Gemini Embedding 2、GPT-4.1 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6 在三项指标上无统计显著差异：GE2 的 R@1=0.804、R@3=0.921、MRR=0.871；GPT-4.1 的 R@1=0.789、R@3=0.915、MRR=0.859；Claude 的 R@1=0.796、R@3=0.903、MRR=0.857。Amazon Nova 2 表现显著较差（R@1=0.673，p<0.0001），可能因使用 GENERIC_INDEX 而非专用的 IMAGE_RETRIEVAL 用途。延迟方面，预计算后 GE2 排序 1,000 条查询仅需 0.66 秒，Nova 2 需 1.16 秒，而 GPT-4.1 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6 分别需要 6,100 秒和 9,415 秒，速度差距达四个数量级。

Conclusion: 原生多模态嵌入与 LLM 联合视觉推理在硬负样本检索任务上达到统计等效的精度水平，但两者具有不同的适用场景：嵌入模型适合大规模、低延迟、固定图库的检索应用（成本可摊销）；LLM 排序器更适合图库频繁更新或规模较小的场景。研究揭示了两种范式的互补优势：嵌入模型独立评分对候选集构成稳健且无位置偏差，而 LLM 能进行跨候选联合推理。作者指出了标题空间硬负样本可能天然偏向嵌入方法、推理条件差异（同时接收 vs 独立评分）、候选集规模限制等局限，并提出将比较扩展至视频和音频模态作为未来方向。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval and classification across different types of media, spanning text, images,video and audio, has traditionally relied on dual-encoder models that align visual and textual representations through contrastive learning. The March 2026 release of Gemini Embedding 2, Google's first natively multimodal embedding model to map text, images, video, audio, and documents into a single shared space, raises competition among multimodal retrieval systems. Simultaneously, frontier Large language models (LLMs) have also demonstrated strong visual understanding, raising the question of whether they can serve as effective zero-shot rankers. Our study provides the first direct comparison of native multimodal embeddings against LLM-based visual ranking on Flickr30k. We observe that GPT-4.1 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 perform on par with Gemini Embedding 2. Additionally, once embeddings are precomputed, multimodal embeddings are better suited for low-latency applications.

</details>


### [136] [Inverse Theory of Mind Modeling for Content Recommendation: From Web Browsing to Dynamic Intelligent Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11354)
*Mengyu Chen,Feiyu Lu,Chun-Fu Chen,Lucas Vinh Tran,Jay Katukuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"逆向心智理论"(Inverse Theory of Mind, IToM)管道，通过五阶段推理从用户行为日志反向推断其信念、偏好与决策风格，进而合成结构化的自然语言用户画像。该方法在 OPeRA 数据集上验证，推理画像在下一动作预测、购物类别预测、大五人格预测和购物态度对齐四项任务中匹配或超越基于访谈的地面真实画像，并通过 VisionOS 空间银行原型展示了跨模态可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统将观察到的交互行为视为稳定偏好的代理，但实际交互常反映探索或比较；随着界面从静态布局向生成式 UI 和扩展现实(XR)演进，亟需更深层、模态无关的用户理解。现有方法存在两类瓶颈：(1) 基于交互的方法(如协同过滤、序列模型)受限于单一行为类型，只能预测同类下一行为；(2) 基于画像的方法虽建模丰富，但依赖访谈、调查等定性数据，成本高且难以规模化。共同缺失的是对用户行为"理由(rationale)"的建模——即为何而行动，而非仅预测下一步动作。

Method: 提出 IToM 五阶段管道，将原始交互事件逐步转化为结构化用户画像：(1) Summarize：将原始事件转为自然语言动作摘要；(2) Percept Reconstruction：基于页面 HTML 重建用户决策上下文，分三层(主选项/次选项/其他选项)标识用户所感知的选择集；(3) Backward Belief Inference：在重建上下文中进行反推信念推理，分动作级和会话级两层生成候选信念(每个动作生成多个竞争假设)；(4) Belief Optimization：使用 Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR, λ=0.3) 在嵌入空间中去除冗余并按异常性质量评分，筛选出 K=50 条多样化信念；(5) Multi-Hypothesis Persona Synthesis：生成 M=7 个多样人格假设，使用置信度加权聚合公式(包含发表的人群先验 π_d 和自适应先验强度 α_d)整合为统一画像。整体使用 Claude Opus 4.6 作为推理引擎，将 LLM 作为近似逆 ToM 推理器，而非推荐器本身。

Result: 在 OPeRA 数据集(46 名用户)上：(1) RQ1 下一动作预测——Claude Opus 4.6 下，推理画像的动作生成准确率达 25.69%，比地面真实画像(17.06%)提升 +50.6% 相对改进；(2) RQ2 购物类别预测——Hit@3=100%，NDCG@5=0.701，比 CoT 基线提升 6.5%；(3) RQ3 大五人格预测——平均 MAE=0.762，优于所有人群基线(0.836)，并将平均 Pearson 相关性从单假设基线的负值(-0.103/-0.008)反转为正值(+0.103)；(4) RQ4 购物态度对齐——归一化准确率 76.6%，优于直接推理(71.9%)和问卷法(72.5%)。案例研究：基于 VisionOS 构建的画像驱动空间银行应用证明，从 2D 浏览行为推断的画像可无缝迁移到 3D 空间界面布局决策中。

Conclusion: IToM 框架有效桥接了基于交互的方法(数据丰富但范围受限)与基于画像的方法(理解丰富但依赖定性数据)之间的鸿沟，通过行为证据反推信念实现纯数据驱动的用户建模。多假设推理是纠正"画像偏差(persona bias)"的关键机制：单一解释将模糊证据映射为刻板人格预测，导致与真实人格反相关，而多假设聚合通过置信度加权将相关性符号反转。核心价值在于：(a) 消除定性数据收集瓶颈，使画像可在生产环境中规模化部署；(b) 产出可解释、可审计的自然语言制品，支持原始点击流无法胜任的下游任务；(c) 模态无关性使画像可跨 2D/3D 界面与生成式 UI 直接迁移。局限：评估局限于单一购物领域、样本量中等(N=46)、预测方差被压缩(对极端用户区分度有限)、情绪稳定性(Emotional Stability)存在系统性误预测。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems treat observed actions as reliable proxies for user preferences, yet interactions often reflect exploration or comparison rather than stable preference expression. As interfaces evolve from static layouts toward generative UIs and immersive extended reality (XR), the need for deeper, modality-agnostic user understanding grows: these adaptive environments must decide not only what to present but where, when, how prominently, and most importantly why a user acts. We propose an Inverse Theory of Mind (IToM) pipeline that reasons backward from observed interactions to infer the beliefs, preferences, and decision-making traits that explain behavior. The pipeline reconstructs each user's decision context, including what was chosen and what alternatives were available, applies LLM-driven counterfactual reasoning to produce evidence-grounded natural-language belief statements, and synthesizes these beliefs through multi-hypothesis abductive inference into a structured user persona. We evaluate on the OPeRA dataset against ground-truth personality assessments, attitudinal surveys, and interview-based personas across four tasks: next action prediction, shopping attitude alignment, Big Five personality inference, and held-out category prediction. Results show that inferred personas match or exceed ground-truth personas and that multi-hypothesis reasoning is essential for accurate personality prediction. We further demonstrate cross-modal transferability with a persona-driven spatial banking application on VisionOS.

</details>


### [137] [From Numbers to Judgment: Specialist LLM Agents and Reinforcement Learning for European Listed Real Estate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11381)
*Pardis Taghavi,Santosh Bhavani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究欧洲上市房地产分析中LLM专业化的问题。提出Larix系统,将16维分析框架映射为8个专家智能体。发现提示级专家分解能提升模块化数值任务(+15.8个百分点),但不能可靠改善判断任务;而使用GRPO对Qwen3.5-9B进行结构化奖励的后训练则显著提升判断任务(+14.2个百分点),且能迁移到未见过的公司和监管框架。结论:提示分解适用于本地化数值执行,而参数适配适用于整合性财务判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲上市房地产是一个收入型资产类别,其规则因跨境监管而异(法国SIIC须强制派息,卢森堡注册的Aroundtown则无此义务;经常性业绩指标在EPRA Earnings、FFO I和EPS之间因监管体系而不同)。将一个监管体系的规则套用到另一个体系的公司不是近似错误,而是机械性确定错误。这种"阈值分类"(thresholds classification)是模型应用前必须先完成的,但LLM即使提取正确数据也可能应用错误规则,且流畅输出使错误难以察觉。这一领域同时考验两种能力:模块化数值操作(如提取与监管体系匹配的指标)和整合性判断(综合会计、监管和风险维度的证据)。现有金融多智能体系统的增益混淆了分解本身与额外的领域指令和模型差异,需要受控实验来明确何时专家分解有效、何时无效。

Method: 方法包含两部分: (1) 受控同模型分解比较。使用Claude Opus 4.8作为固定前沿模型,在19家覆盖7类监管体系的公司上,比较三种提示条件:通用单体(Claude-Mono)、注入完整16维框架的单体(Claude-Full)、路由至任务对应专家的专家提示(Claude-Spec)。所有条件下源证据、任务指令、输出schema和评分均固定,采用K=4的冻结模板调度。基准分为数值任务(T1/T3/T6:监管匹配的指标、隐含cap rate、派息监管分类)和判断任务(T2/T5:对账调整、契约应力评估)。评分采用确定性字段加权得分,无效输出为零。 (2) 结构化奖励RL后训练。使用GRPO(Group Relative Policy Optimization)在195条真实+5条仅用于训练的反事实行上后训练Qwen3.5-9B,采用rank-32 LoRA适配器,veRL+ vLLM在单H100上实现。奖励直接采用评估所用的确定性任务得分(即任务对齐而非模型评判器),训练30步后保留step-20检查点。评估包含三个互斥的留出集:未见公司(35行)、未见监管体系(25行)、后期时段(45行,反记忆化检查)。

Result: 在19家公司主基准上:(1) 专家分解提升数值聚合得分从78.9%到94.7%(+15.8个百分点,配对McNemar p=0.012),4次冻结模板调度均为正向,但判断聚合保持不变(47.4%,p=1.0);专家条件较完整框架单体对照在T1领先36.8分、T3领先21.1分,T2落后15.8分,表明是作用域化的专家角色而非框架披露驱动了数值增益。(2) Qwen3.5-9B经GRPO后训练在25行开发集上从72.2%提升至84.2%(+12.0),判断聚合提升14.2分,所有四个未达天花板任务均有改善。(3) 留出集迁移:未见公司+15.2分总体、T5契约应力+40.4分;未见监管体系+4.3分;后期时段+2.5分。奖励遥测未出现"总是预测违约"的退化(仅5.5% rollout触发)。T1/T3在95格扩展基准上8B模型接近底线,反映参数化回忆能力而非后训练的边界。

Conclusion: 本文提出"任务依赖的设计原则":提示级分解适用于简化本地化、可验证的财务操作;当可靠性能依赖于整合多个财务证据源时,则采用有针对性的参数适配。两种专业化形式(提示分解 vs. 参数后训练)解决不同瓶颈,应协同使用。结论受限于基准规模(19家公司、7类体系、3个专家、5个任务)、单一前沿模型家族和单一9B开源模型、单一保留检查点、未评估的下游合成层。

Abstract: We study whether the localized numerical operations and integrative judgments of financial analysis benefit from the same form of LLM specialization. Larix maps a 16-lens European listed-real-estate analysis framework to eight lens-aligned specialists; we compare a frontier LLM under monolithic versus specialist-decomposed prompting while holding the model, source evidence, task instructions, output schema, and scoring fixed. Across 19 firms spanning seven regulatory wrappers, decomposition improves the numerical-task aggregate by 15.8 percentage points but does not reliably improve, and can reduce, performance on judgment tasks, a pattern stable across four frozen-template dispatches; a single-agent control given the complete framework does not reproduce the numerical gain. Post-training Qwen3.5-9B with GRPO using task-aligned structured rewards then raises the development-split score by 12.0 points and the judgment aggregate by 14.2 points, with gains on all four sub-ceiling tasks; the gains transfer to unseen firms (+15.2 points overall; +40.4 on covenant stress) and to unseen regulatory wrappers (+4.3), with positive transfer on all three anti-memorization splits. Prompt-level decomposition thus improves modular numerical execution, whereas targeted parameter adaptation improves integrative financial judgment.

</details>


### [138] [When Self-Consistency Backfires: Majority Vote Hurts the Majority of Hard Science Problems for Small LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11403)
*Utkarsh Bahuguna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过预注册的验证性实验证明,在GPQA Diamond这一高难度科学基准上,对小型指令微调LLM(Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3-8B)使用自一致性多数投票时,反而会降低大多数问题的准确率(Qwen 56.6%,Llama 65.7%);且由于模型置信度与正确性脱钩,无论是多数一致性门控还是token熵门控,都无法在不依赖外部验证器的情况下恢复投票所损失的理论精度空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自一致性(SC)多数投票被广泛视为推理时计算的"低风险"准确率提升手段,其隐含假设是"多采样多投票至少不会变差"。然而在困难问题上,若模型的采样概率集中在错误答案上,更多采样只会固化错误投票,即所谓"回火(backfire)"现象。论文旨在以预注册方式量化这一负面效应在完整困难基准上发生的频率,并检验是否存在廉价的、无需验证器的信号(一致性比率、token熵)可用于判断"何时投票、何时跳过",以避免回火损失。

Method: 采用预注册的验证性设计:数据集为完整GPQA Diamond基准(198道研究生级多选题),分为47道探索集和151道验证集(假设在分析前已锁定并打git标签)。模型为Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-Turbo和Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct-Lite,每题采样N=64条链式推理,温度0.7。指标MV_acc(N)通过对子集无替换蒙特卡洛采样估计;回火定义为mv_gain = MV_acc(64)−MV_acc(1) < 0。比较方法包括:(1)固定预算投票基线;(2)网格oracle(逐题在{1,2,4,8,16,32,64}中选取最佳N,需真值,作为理论上限);(3)一致性门控(k=8,τ=0.75);(4)基于k=4探针样本的平均token熵门控。不确定性通过问题级bootstrap(1000次)报告95%置信区间。

Result: 四个预注册假设全部通过。回火发生在多数问题上:Qwen合并池56.6% [49.5,63.6],Llama 65.7% [59.1,71.7];验证集上Qwen 60.3%、Llama 65.6%。尽管逐题层面广泛受损,聚合层面准确率仅微升(Qwen 0.342→0.369,Llama 0.273→0.313),呈现"均值平稳但个体受损"的偏差。领域分布显示化学题回火最严重(两模型均为~67-69%)。网格oracle表明理论上限比N=1高14(Qwen)至17(Llama)个百分点。然而,一致性门控相对N=64固定投票仅带来0.0006(Qwen)和0.0014(Llama)的差异,熵门控类似——两者均未实质性回收oracle空间。机制层面,最高一致性区间内多数答案仅约52.5%正确(Qwen),Llama更降至28.6%,且低于其最低一致性区间,表明置信度无法预测正确性。

Conclusion: 在困难推理任务上,自一致性多数投票会损害多数问题,且廉价的、无需验证器的一致性或熵信号无法可靠识别何时投票有利。要回收oracle所揭示的精度空间,需要模型外部信号(如训练型验证器、过程奖励模型PRM)。论文明确将"推理原生(reasoning-native)模型是否也存在或更少存在此回火现象"标注为核心开放问题。局限性包括:Llama接近随机基线、单一基准、仅两个小型非推理模型、熵门控阈值在样本内选取可能导致乐观估计、门控信号种类有限(未测最终答案对数概率边际等)。

Abstract: Self-consistency (SC) via majority vote is a widely used way to spend inference-time compute: sample N chains of thought, return the plurality answer. On the full GPQA Diamond benchmark (198 graduate-level science questions), majority voting reduces per-problem accuracy on a majority of problems for two instruction-tuned models from different families: 56.6% of problems for Qwen2.5-7B and 65.7% for Llama-3-8B, with Qwen the primary demonstration and Llama corroborating the direction from a near-chance baseline. The effect was pre-registered on a 151-problem confirmatory split after being observed on 47 exploratory problems, and all four confirmatory hypotheses passed. A grid oracle that routes each problem to the best N across {1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64} marks a theoretical upper bound 14 accuracy points above N = 1 for Qwen and 17 for Llama, an oracle bound requiring ground truth rather than a deployable method. No verifier-free gate reaches it: neither a plurality-agreement gate nor a token-entropy gate moves accuracy more than 0.002 from fixed-budget voting at N = 64. The mechanism is direct: confidence does not track correctness on these problems. In the highest-agreement bin the plurality answer is correct about half the time for Qwen, and for Llama that bin is less accurate than its lowest-agreement bin. We pre-register and confirm these findings on small instruction-tuned models; we do not test reasoning-native models, which we flag as the central open question.

</details>


### [139] [Social Chain of Thought: A Multi-Agent Architecture Grounded in Medical Differential Diagnosis Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11420)
*Del Coburn,Scott Sanner,Dan Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Social Chain of Thought (SCoT)"的多轮多智能体管道,用于医学鉴别诊断,通过构建审议式协作推理框架,提升LLM在复杂医疗诊断场景中的召回率表现,尤其在困难病例上显著优于单体推理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ChatGPT等LLM在医疗相关对话中占比超过5%,医疗诊断推理的透明度成为重要设计关切。现有研究虽提出多智能体诊断方法,但缺乏系统分析:何时需要多智能体系统、为何有帮助、在哪些场景优于单体推理。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出Social Chain of Thought (SCoT)——一种面向医学鉴别诊断的多轮多智能体管道,将多智能体交互结构化为协作式LLM审议推理框架。评估方案包括:与单智能体基线对比、单智能体管道消融实验,以及best-of-n扩展策略对比,系统验证多智能体审议机制的有效性。

Result: SCoT相较于单体推理展现出显著的召回率优势,该优势无法通过单纯扩展单体推理(best-of-n)来复现。在最困难的诊断案例中,多轮专科智能体对话能够有效恢复真实诊断,收敛于高召回率的鉴别诊断结果。

Conclusion: 多智能体审议机制在医学鉴别诊断中具有不可替代的价值,尤其适用于需要整合多种专科推理的复杂困难病例;SCoT的召回优势并非源自推理量的简单扩展,而源于多轮专科对话的协作审议过程,为医疗LLM系统设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Medical diagnostic reasoning is a high-impact use case for LLMs that carries significant implications for the health and wellbeing of users. When OpenAI (2026) reports that more than 5% of ChatGPT messages globally are healthcare-related, the transparency of these systems becomes a serious design concern. This is especially true for complex cases, where differential diagnosis often requires integrating multiple forms of specialist reasoning.
